    def clone(self, database: str) -> 'GraphDriver':
        """
        Returns a shallow copy of this driver with a different default database.
        Reuses the same connection (e.g. FalkorDB, Neo4j).
        """
        if database == self._database:
            cloned = self
        elif database == self.default_group_id:
            cloned = FalkorDriver(falkor_db=self.client)
        else:
            # Create a new instance of FalkorDriver with the same connection but a different database
            cloned = FalkorDriver(falkor_db=self.client, database=database)

## Using Graphiti with Ollama (Local LLM)

Graphiti supports Ollama for running local LLMs and embedding models via Ollama's OpenAI-compatible API. This is ideal
for privacy-focused applications or when you want to avoid API costs.

**Note:** Use `OpenAIGenericClient` (not `OpenAIClient`) for Ollama and other OpenAI-compatible providers like LM
Studio. The `OpenAIGenericClient` is optimized for local models with a higher default max token limit (16K vs 8K) and
full support for structured outputs.
[31;1mInvalidOperation: [31;1mCannot set property. Property setting is supported only on core types in this language mode.[0m

 succeeded in 557ms:

### Using Ollama for Local LLM

To use Ollama with the MCP server, configure it as an OpenAI-compatible endpoint:

```yaml
llm:
  provider: "openai"
  model: "gpt-oss:120b"  # or your preferred Ollama model
  api_base: "http://localhost:11434/v1"
  api_key: "ollama"  # dummy key required

embedder:
  provider: "sentence_transformers"  # recommended for local setup
  model: "all-MiniLM-L6-v2"
```

Make sure Ollama is running locally with: `ollama serve`

**Ollama (local):**
- Hardware dependent → `SEMAPHORE_LIMIT=1-5`
- Monitor CPU/GPU usage and adjust

#### Symptoms

- **Too high**: 429 rate limit errors, increased API costs from parallel processing
- **Too low**: Slow episode throughput, underutilized API quota

#### Monitoring

- Watch logs for `429` rate limit errors
- Monitor episode processing times in server logs
- Check your LLM provider's dashboard for actual request rates
- Track token usage and costs

    def create(config: LLMConfig) -> LLMClient:
        """Create an LLM client based on the configured provider."""
        import logging

        logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

        provider = config.provider.lower()

        match provider:
            case 'openai':
                if not config.providers.openai:
                    raise ValueError('OpenAI provider configuration not found')

                api_key = config.providers.openai.api_key
                _validate_api_key('OpenAI', api_key, logger)

                from graphiti_core.llm_client.config import LLMConfig as CoreLLMConfig

                # Use the same model for both main and small model slots
                small_model = config.model

                llm_config = CoreLLMConfig(
                    api_key=api_key,
                    model=config.model,
                    small_model=small_model,
                    temperature=config.temperature,
                    max_tokens=config.max_tokens,
                )

                # Check if this is a reasoning model (o1, o3, gpt-5 family)
                reasoning_prefixes = ('o1', 'o3', 'gpt-5')
                is_reasoning_model = config.model.startswith(reasoning_prefixes)

                # Only pass reasoning/verbosity parameters for reasoning models (gpt-5 family)
                if is_reasoning_model:
                    return OpenAIClient(config=llm_config, reasoning='minimal', verbosity='low')
                else:
                    # For non-reasoning models, explicitly pass None to disable these parameters
                    return OpenAIClient(config=llm_config, reasoning=None, verbosity=None)

            case 'azure_openai':
                if not HAS_AZURE_LLM:

    @staticmethod
    def create(config: EmbedderConfig) -> EmbedderClient:
        """Create an Embedder client based on the configured provider."""
        import logging

        logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

        provider = config.provider.lower()

        match provider:
            case 'openai':
                if not config.providers.openai:
                    raise ValueError('OpenAI provider configuration not found')

                api_key = config.providers.openai.api_key
                _validate_api_key('OpenAI Embedder', api_key, logger)

                from graphiti_core.embedder.openai import OpenAIEmbedderConfig

                embedder_config = OpenAIEmbedderConfig(
                    api_key=api_key,
                    embedding_model=config.model,
                    base_url=config.providers.openai.api_url,  # Support custom endpoints like Ollama
                    embedding_dim=config.dimensions,  # Support custom embedding dimensions
                )
                return OpenAIEmbedder(config=embedder_config)

            case 'azure_openai':
                if not HAS_AZURE_EMBEDDER:
                    raise ValueError(
                        'Azure OpenAI embedder not available in current graphiti-core version'
                    )
                if not config.providers.azure_openai:

from graphiti_core.driver.driver import GraphProvider
from graphiti_core.helpers import semaphore_gather
from graphiti_core.search.search_config import SearchResults

F = TypeVar('F', bound=Callable[..., Awaitable[Any]])


def handle_multiple_group_ids(func: F) -> F:
    """
    Decorator for FalkorDB methods that need to handle multiple group_ids.
    Runs the function for each group_id separately and merges results.
    """

    @functools.wraps(func)
    async def wrapper(self, *args, **kwargs):
        group_ids_func_pos = get_parameter_position(func, 'group_ids')
        group_ids_pos = (
            group_ids_func_pos - 1 if group_ids_func_pos is not None else None
        )  # Adjust for zero-based index
        group_ids = kwargs.get('group_ids')

        # If not in kwargs and position exists, get from args
        if group_ids is None and group_ids_pos is not None and len(args) > group_ids_pos:
            group_ids = args[group_ids_pos]

        # Only handle FalkorDB with multiple group_ids
        if (
            hasattr(self, 'clients')
            and hasattr(self.clients, 'driver')
            and self.clients.driver.provider == GraphProvider.FALKORDB
            and group_ids
            and len(group_ids) > 1
        ):
            # Execute for each group_id concurrently
            driver = self.clients.driver

            async def execute_for_group(gid: str):
                # Remove group_ids from args if it was passed positionally
                filtered_args = list(args)
                if group_ids_pos is not None and len(args) > group_ids_pos:
                    filtered_args.pop(group_ids_pos)

                return await func(
                    self,
                    *filtered_args,
                    **{**kwargs, 'group_ids': [gid], 'driver': driver.clone(database=gid)},
                )

            results = await semaphore_gather(
                *[execute_for_group(gid) for gid in group_ids],
                max_coroutines=getattr(self, 'max_coroutines', None),
            )

            # Merge results based on type
            if isinstance(results[0], SearchResults):
                return SearchResults.merge(results)
            elif isinstance(results[0], list):
                return [item for result in results for item in result]
            elif isinstance(results[0], tuple):
[31;1mInvalidOperation: [31;1mCannot set property. Property setting is supported only on core types in this language mode.[0m

 succeeded in 571ms:
        # Clean up multiple spaces
        sanitized = ' '.join(sanitized.split())
        return sanitized

    def build_fulltext_query(
        self, query: str, group_ids: list[str] | None = None, max_query_length: int = 128
    ) -> str:
        """
        Build a fulltext query string for FalkorDB using RedisSearch syntax.
        FalkorDB uses RedisSearch-like syntax where:
        - Field queries use @ prefix: @field:value
        - Multiple values for same field: (@field:value1|value2)
        - Text search doesn't need @ prefix for content fields
        - AND is implicit with space: (@group_id:value) (text)
        - OR uses pipe within parentheses: (@group_id:value1|value2)
        """
        validate_group_ids(group_ids)

        if group_ids is None or len(group_ids) == 0:
            group_filter = ''
        else:
            # Escape group_ids with quotes to prevent RediSearch syntax errors
            # with reserved words like "main" or special characters like hyphens
            escaped_group_ids = [f'"{gid}"' for gid in group_ids]
            group_values = '|'.join(escaped_group_ids)
            group_filter = f'(@group_id:{group_values})'

        sanitized_query = self.sanitize(query)

        # Remove stopwords and empty tokens from the sanitized query
        query_words = sanitized_query.split()
        filtered_words = [word for word in query_words if word and word.lower() not in STOPWORDS]
        sanitized_query = ' | '.join(filtered_words)

        # If the query is too long return no query
        if len(sanitized_query.split(' ')) + len(group_ids or '') >= max_query_length:
            return ''

        full_query = group_filter + ' (' + sanitized_query + ')'

        return full_query

        Notes
        -----
        This method performs several steps including node extraction, edge extraction,
        deduplication, and database updates. It also handles embedding generation
        and edge invalidation.

        It is recommended to run this method as a background process, such as in a queue.
        It's important that each episode is added sequentially and awaited before adding
        the next one. For web applications, consider using FastAPI's background tasks
        or a dedicated task queue like Celery for this purpose.

        Example using FastAPI background tasks:
            @app.post("/add_episode")
            async def add_episode_endpoint(episode_data: EpisodeData):
                background_tasks.add_task(graphiti.add_episode, **episode_data.dict())
                return {"message": "Episode processing started"}
        """
        start = time()
        now = utc_now()

        validate_entity_types(entity_types)
        validate_excluded_entity_types(excluded_entity_types, entity_types)

        if group_id is None:
            # if group_id is None, use the default group id by the provider
            # and the preset database name will be used
            group_id = get_default_group_id(self.driver.provider)
        else:
            validate_group_id(group_id)
            if group_id != self.driver._database:
                # if group_id is provided, use it as the database name
                self.driver = self.driver.clone(database=group_id)
                self.clients.driver = self.driver

        with self.tracer.start_span('add_episode') as span:
            try:
                # Retrieve previous episodes for context
                previous_episodes = (
                    await self.retrieve_episodes(
                        reference_time,
                        last_n=RELEVANT_SCHEMA_LIMIT,
                        group_ids=[group_id],
                        source=source,
                    )
                    if previous_episode_uuids is None
                    else await EpisodicNode.get_by_uuids(self.driver, previous_episode_uuids)
                )

                # Get or create episode
                episode = (
                    await EpisodicNode.get_by_uuid(self.driver, uuid)
                    if uuid is not None
                    else EpisodicNode(
                        name=name,
                        group_id=group_id,
                        labels=[],
                        source=source,
                        content=episode_body,
                        source_description=source_description,
                        created_at=now,
                        valid_at=reference_time,
                    )
                )

                # Create default edge type map
                edge_type_map_default = (
                    {('Entity', 'Entity'): list(edge_types.keys())}
                    if edge_types is not None
                    else {('Entity', 'Entity'): []}
                )

                # Extract and resolve nodes
                extracted_nodes, node_episode_index_map = await extract_nodes(
                    self.clients,
                    episode,
                    previous_episodes,
                    entity_types,
                    excluded_entity_types,
                    custom_extraction_instructions,
                )

                nodes, uuid_map, _ = await resolve_extracted_nodes(
                    self.clients,
                    extracted_nodes,
                    episode,
                    previous_episodes,
                    entity_types,
                )

 succeeded in 577ms:
        -----
        This method performs several steps including:
        - Saving all episodes to the database
        - Retrieving previous episode context for each new episode
        - Extracting nodes and edges from all episodes
        - Generating embeddings for nodes and edges
        - Deduplicating nodes and edges
        - Saving nodes, episodic edges, and entity edges to the knowledge graph

        This bulk operation is designed for efficiency when processing multiple episodes
        at once. However, it's important to ensure that the bulk operation doesn't
        overwhelm system resources. Consider implementing rate limiting or chunking for
        very large batches of episodes.

        Edge invalidation and date extraction (``valid_at`` / ``invalid_at``) are
        performed in the bulk path as well: edges flow through ``extract_edges`` and
        ``resolve_extracted_edges`` just like in ``add_episode``, and any invalidated
        edges are persisted alongside the newly resolved ones.
        """
        with self.tracer.start_span('add_episode_bulk') as bulk_span:
            bulk_span.add_attributes({'episode.count': len(bulk_episodes)})

            try:
                start = time()
                now = utc_now()

                # if group_id is None, use the default group id by the provider
                if group_id is None:
                    group_id = get_default_group_id(self.driver.provider)
                else:
                    validate_group_id(group_id)
                    if group_id != self.driver._database:
                        # if group_id is provided, use it as the database name
                        self.driver = self.driver.clone(database=group_id)
                        self.clients.driver = self.driver

                # Create default edge type map
                edge_type_map_default = (
                    {('Entity', 'Entity'): list(edge_types.keys())}
                    if edge_types is not None
                    else {('Entity', 'Entity'): []}
                )

                episodes = [
                    await EpisodicNode.get_by_uuid(self.driver, episode.uuid)
                    if episode.uuid is not None
                    else EpisodicNode(
                        name=episode.name,
                        labels=[],
                        source=episode.source,
                        content=episode.content,
                        source_description=episode.source_description,
                        group_id=group_id,
                        created_at=now,
                        valid_at=episode.reference_time,
                    )
                    for episode in bulk_episodes
                ]

                # Save all episodes
                await add_nodes_and_edges_bulk(
                    driver=self.driver,
                    episodic_nodes=episodes,
                    episodic_edges=[],
                    entity_nodes=[],
                    entity_edges=[],
                    embedder=self.embedder,
                )

                # Get previous episode context for each episode
                episode_context = await retrieve_previous_episodes_bulk(self.driver, episodes)

                # Extract and dedupe nodes and edges
                (
                    nodes_by_episode,
                    uuid_map,
                    extracted_edges_bulk,
                ) = await self._extract_and_dedupe_nodes_bulk(
                    episode_context,
                    edge_type_map or edge_type_map_default,
                    edge_types,
                    entity_types,
                    excluded_entity_types,
                    custom_extraction_instructions,
                )

                # Create Episodic Edges
                episodic_edges: list[EpisodicEdge] = []
                for episode_uuid, nodes in nodes_by_episode.items():
                    episodic_edges.extend(build_episodic_edges(nodes, episode_uuid, now))

            uuid=uuid or None,  # Ensure None is passed if uuid is None

        logger.error(f'Error queuing episode: {error_msg}')

@mcp.tool()
async def search_nodes(
    query: str,
    group_ids: list[str] | None = None,
    max_nodes: int = 10,
    entity_types: list[str] | None = None,
) -> NodeSearchResponse | ErrorResponse:
    """Search for nodes in the graph memory.

    Args:
        query: The search query
        group_ids: Optional list of group IDs to filter results
        max_nodes: Maximum number of nodes to return (default: 10)
        entity_types: Optional list of entity type names to filter by
    """
    global graphiti_service

    if graphiti_service is None:
        return ErrorResponse(error='Graphiti service not initialized')

    try:
        client = await graphiti_service.get_client()

        # Use the provided group_ids or fall back to the default from config if none provided
        effective_group_ids = (
            group_ids
            if group_ids is not None
            else [config.graphiti.group_id]
            if config.graphiti.group_id
            else []
        )

        # Create search filters
        search_filters = SearchFilters(
            node_labels=entity_types,
        )

        # Use the search_ method with node search config
        from graphiti_core.search.search_config_recipes import NODE_HYBRID_SEARCH_RRF

        results = await client.search_(
            query=query,
            config=NODE_HYBRID_SEARCH_RRF,
            group_ids=effective_group_ids,
            search_filter=search_filters,
        )

        # Extract nodes from results
        nodes = results.nodes[:max_nodes] if results.nodes else []

        if not nodes:
            return NodeSearchResponse(message='No relevant nodes found', nodes=[])

    @handle_multiple_group_ids
    async def search(
        self,
        query: str,
        center_node_uuid: str | None = None,
        group_ids: list[str] | None = None,
        num_results=DEFAULT_SEARCH_LIMIT,
        search_filter: SearchFilters | None = None,
        driver: GraphDriver | None = None,
    ) -> list[EntityEdge]:
        """
        Perform a hybrid search on the knowledge graph.

        This method executes a search query on the graph, combining vector and
        text-based search techniques to retrieve relevant facts, returning the edges as a string.

        This is our basic out-of-the-box search, for more robust results we recommend using our more advanced
        search method graphiti.search_().

        Parameters
        ----------
        query : str
            The search query string.
        center_node_uuid: str, optional
            Facts will be reranked based on proximity to this node
        group_ids : list[str | None] | None, optional
            The graph partitions to return data from.
        num_results : int, optional
            The maximum number of results to return. Defaults to 10.

        Returns
        -------
        list
            A list of EntityEdge objects that are relevant to the search query.

        Notes
        -----
        This method uses a SearchConfig with num_episodes set to 0 and
        num_results set to the provided num_results parameter.

        The search is performed using the current date and time as the reference
        point for temporal relevance.
        """
        search_config = (
            EDGE_HYBRID_SEARCH_RRF if center_node_uuid is None else EDGE_HYBRID_SEARCH_NODE_DISTANCE
        )
        search_config.limit = num_results

        edges = (
            await search(
                self.clients,
                query,
                group_ids,
                search_config,
                search_filter if search_filter is not None else SearchFilters(),
                driver=driver,
                center_node_uuid=center_node_uuid,
            )
        ).edges

        return edges

    async def _search(
        self,

        config: SearchConfig,
        group_ids: list[str] | None = None,
        center_node_uuid: str | None = None,
        bfs_origin_node_uuids: list[str] | None = None,
        search_filter: SearchFilters | None = None,
    ) -> SearchResults:
        """DEPRECATED"""
        return await self.search_(
            query, config, group_ids, center_node_uuid, bfs_origin_node_uuids, search_filter
        )

    @handle_multiple_group_ids
    async def search_(
        self,
        query: str,
        config: SearchConfig = COMBINED_HYBRID_SEARCH_CROSS_ENCODER,
        group_ids: list[str] | None = None,
        center_node_uuid: str | None = None,
        bfs_origin_node_uuids: list[str] | None = None,
        search_filter: SearchFilters | None = None,
        driver: GraphDriver | None = None,
    ) -> SearchResults:
        """search_ (replaces _search) is our advanced search method that returns Graph objects (nodes and edges) rather
        than a list of facts. This endpoint allows the end user to utilize more advanced features such as filters and
        different search and reranker methodologies across different layers in the graph.

        For different config recipes refer to search/search_config_recipes.
        """

        return await search(
            self.clients,
            query,
            group_ids,
            config,
            search_filter if search_filter is not None else SearchFilters(),
            center_node_uuid,
            bfs_origin_node_uuids,
            driver=driver,
        )

        entity_types: Any,
        uuid: str | None,

        """Add an episode for processing.

        Args:
            group_id: The group ID for the episode
            name: Name of the episode
            content: Episode content
            source_description: Description of the episode source
            episode_type: Type of the episode
            entity_types: Entity types for extraction
            uuid: Episode UUID

        Returns:
            The position in the queue
        """

            raise RuntimeError('Queue service not initialized. Call initialize() first.')

        async def process_episode():
            """Process the episode using the graphiti client."""
            try:
                logger.info(f'Processing episode {uuid} for group {group_id}')

                # Process the episode using the graphiti client
                await self._graphiti_client.add_episode(
                    name=name,
                    episode_body=content,
                    source_description=source_description,
                    source=episode_type,
                    group_id=group_id,
                    reference_time=datetime.now(timezone.utc),
                    entity_types=entity_types,
                    uuid=uuid,
                )

                logger.info(f'Successfully processed episode {uuid} for group {group_id}')

                logger.error(f'Failed to process episode {uuid} for group {group_id}: {str(e)}')

def get_default_group_id(provider: GraphProvider) -> str:
    """
    This function differentiates the default group id based on the database type.
    For most databases, the default group id is an empty string, while there are database types that require a specific default group id.
    """
    if provider == GraphProvider.FALKORDB:
        return '\\_'
    else:
        return ''

29:def handle_multiple_group_ids(func: F) -> F:
30:    """
31:    Decorator for FalkorDB methods that need to handle multiple group_ids.
32:    Runs the function for each group_id separately and merges results.
33:    """
34:
35:    @functools.wraps(func)
36:    async def wrapper(self, *args, **kwargs):
37:        group_ids_func_pos = get_parameter_position(func, 'group_ids')
38:        group_ids_pos = (
39:            group_ids_func_pos - 1 if group_ids_func_pos is not None else None
40:        )  # Adjust for zero-based index
41:        group_ids = kwargs.get('group_ids')
42:
43:        # If not in kwargs and position exists, get from args
44:        if group_ids is None and group_ids_pos is not None and len(args) > group_ids_pos:
45:            group_ids = args[group_ids_pos]
46:
47:        # Only handle FalkorDB with multiple group_ids
48:        if (
49:            hasattr(self, 'clients')
50:            and hasattr(self.clients, 'driver')
51:            and self.clients.driver.provider == GraphProvider.FALKORDB
52:            and group_ids
53:            and len(group_ids) > 1
54:        ):
55:            # Execute for each group_id concurrently
56:            driver = self.clients.driver
57:
58:            async def execute_for_group(gid: str):
59:                # Remove group_ids from args if it was passed positionally
60:                filtered_args = list(args)
61:                if group_ids_pos is not None and len(args) > group_ids_pos:
62:                    filtered_args.pop(group_ids_pos)
63:
64:                return await func(
65:                    self,
66:                    *filtered_args,
67:                    **{**kwargs, 'group_ids': [gid], 'driver': driver.clone(database=gid)},
68:                )
69:
70:            results = await semaphore_gather(
71:                *[execute_for_group(gid) for gid in group_ids],
72:                max_coroutines=getattr(self, 'max_coroutines', None),
73:            )
74:
75:            # Merge results based on type
76:            if isinstance(results[0], SearchResults):
77:                return SearchResults.merge(results)
78:            elif isinstance(results[0], list):
79:                return [item for result in results for item in result]
80:            elif isinstance(results[0], tuple):
81:                # Handle tuple outputs (like build_communities returning (nodes, edges))
82:                merged_tuple = []
83:                for i in range(len(results[0])):
84:                    component_results = [result[i] for result in results]
85:                    if isinstance(component_results[0], list):
86:                        merged_tuple.append(
87:                            [item for component in component_results for item in component]
88:                        )
89:                    else:
90:                        merged_tuple.append(component_results)
91:                return tuple(merged_tuple)
92:            else:
93:                return results
94:
95:        # Normal execution
96:        return await func(self, *args, **kwargs)
97:
98:    return wrapper  # type: ignore
99:

101:def get_parameter_position(func: Callable, param_name: str) -> int | None:
102:    """
103:    Returns the positional index of a parameter in the function signature.
104:    If the parameter is not found, returns None.
105:    """
106:    sig = inspect.signature(func)
107:    for idx, (name, _param) in enumerate(sig.parameters.items()):
108:        if name == param_name:
109:            return idx
110:    return None

75:class FalkorDriverSession(GraphDriverSession):
76:    provider = GraphProvider.FALKORDB
77:
78:    def __init__(self, graph: FalkorGraph):
79:        self.graph = graph
80:
81:    async def __aenter__(self):
82:        return self
83:
84:    async def __aexit__(self, exc_type, exc, tb):
85:        # No cleanup needed for Falkor, but method must exist
86:        pass
87:
88:    async def close(self):
89:        # No explicit close needed for FalkorDB, but method must exist
90:        pass
91:
92:    async def execute_write(self, func, *args, **kwargs):
93:        # Directly await the provided async function with `self` as the transaction/session
94:        return await func(self, *args, **kwargs)
95:
96:    async def run(self, query: str | list, **kwargs: Any) -> Any:
97:        # FalkorDB does not support argument for Label Set, so it's converted into an array of queries
98:        if isinstance(query, list):
99:            for cypher, params in query:
100:                params = convert_datetimes_to_strings(params)
101:                await self.graph.query(str(cypher), params)  # type: ignore[reportUnknownArgumentType]
102:        else:
103:            params = dict(kwargs)
104:            params = convert_datetimes_to_strings(params)
105:            await self.graph.query(str(query), params)  # type: ignore[reportUnknownArgumentType]
106:        # Assuming `graph.query` is async (ideal); otherwise, wrap in executor
107:        return None

110:class FalkorDriver(GraphDriver):
111:    provider = GraphProvider.FALKORDB
112:    default_group_id: str = '\\_'
113:    fulltext_syntax: str = '@'  # FalkorDB uses a redisearch-like syntax for fulltext queries
114:    aoss_client: None = None
115:
116:    def __init__(
117:        self,
118:        host: str = 'localhost',
119:        port: int = 6379,
120:        username: str | None = None,
121:        password: str | None = None,
122:        falkor_db: FalkorDB | None = None,
123:        database: str = 'default_db',
124:    ):
125:        """
126:        Initialize the FalkorDB driver.
127:
128:        FalkorDB is a multi-tenant graph database.
129:        To connect, provide the host and port.
130:        The default parameters assume a local (on-premises) FalkorDB instance.
131:
132:        Args:
133:        host (str): The host where FalkorDB is running.
134:        port (int): The port on which FalkorDB is listening.
135:        username (str | None): The username for authentication (if required).
136:        password (str | None): The password for authentication (if required).
137:        falkor_db (FalkorDB | None): An existing FalkorDB instance to use instead of creating a new one.
138:        database (str): The name of the database to connect to. Defaults to 'default_db'.
139:        """
140:        super().__init__()
141:        self._database = database
142:        if falkor_db is not None:
143:            # If a FalkorDB instance is provided, use it directly
144:            self.client = falkor_db
145:        else:
146:            self.client = FalkorDB(host=host, port=port, username=username, password=password)
147:
148:        # Instantiate FalkorDB operations
149:        self._entity_node_ops = FalkorEntityNodeOperations()
150:        self._episode_node_ops = FalkorEpisodeNodeOperations()
151:        self._community_node_ops = FalkorCommunityNodeOperations()
152:        self._saga_node_ops = FalkorSagaNodeOperations()
153:        self._entity_edge_ops = FalkorEntityEdgeOperations()
154:        self._episodic_edge_ops = FalkorEpisodicEdgeOperations()
155:        self._community_edge_ops = FalkorCommunityEdgeOperations()
156:        self._has_episode_edge_ops = FalkorHasEpisodeEdgeOperations()
157:        self._next_episode_edge_ops = FalkorNextEpisodeEdgeOperations()
158:        self._search_ops = FalkorSearchOperations()
159:        self._graph_ops = FalkorGraphMaintenanceOperations()
160:
161:        # Schedule the indices and constraints to be built
162:        try:
163:            # Try to get the current event loop
164:            loop = asyncio.get_running_loop()
165:            # Schedule the build_indices_and_constraints to run
166:            loop.create_task(self.build_indices_and_constraints())
167:        except RuntimeError:
168:            # No event loop running, this will be handled later
169:            pass
170:
171:    # --- Operations properties ---
172:
173:    @property
174:    def entity_node_ops(self) -> EntityNodeOperations:
175:        return self._entity_node_ops
176:
177:    @property
178:    def episode_node_ops(self) -> EpisodeNodeOperations:
179:        return self._episode_node_ops

183:        return self._community_node_ops

187:        return self._saga_node_ops

191:        return self._entity_edge_ops

194:    def episodic_edge_ops(self) -> EpisodicEdgeOperations:
195:        return self._episodic_edge_ops

203:        return self._has_episode_edge_ops

214:    def graph_ops(self) -> GraphMaintenanceOperations:

216:
217:    def _get_graph(self, graph_name: str | None) -> FalkorGraph:
218:        # FalkorDB requires a non-None database name for multi-tenant graphs; the default is "default_db"
219:        if graph_name is None:
220:            graph_name = self._database
221:        return self.client.select_graph(graph_name)
222:
223:    async def execute_query(self, cypher_query_, **kwargs: Any):
224:        graph = self._get_graph(self._database)
225:
226:        # Convert datetime objects to ISO strings (FalkorDB does not support datetime objects directly)
227:        params = convert_datetimes_to_strings(dict(kwargs))
228:
229:        try:
230:            result = await graph.query(cypher_query_, params)  # type: ignore[reportUnknownArgumentType]
231:        except Exception as e:
232:            if 'already indexed' in str(e):
233:                # check if index already exists
234:                logger.info(f'Index already exists: {e}')
235:                return None
236:            logger.error(f'Error executing FalkorDB query: {e}\n{cypher_query_}\n{params}')
237:            raise
238:
239:        # Convert the result header to a list of strings
240:        header = [h[1] for h in result.header]
241:
242:        # Convert FalkorDB's result format (list of lists) to the format expected by Graphiti (list of dicts)
243:        records = []
244:        for row in result.result_set:
245:            record = {}
246:            for i, field_name in enumerate(header):
247:                if i < len(row):
248:                    record[field_name] = row[i]
249:                else:
250:                    # If there are more fields in header than values in row, set to None
251:                    record[field_name] = None
252:            records.append(record)
253:
254:        return records, header, None
255:
256:    def session(self, database: str | None = None) -> GraphDriverSession:
257:        return FalkorDriverSession(self._get_graph(database))
258:
259:    async def close(self) -> None:
260:        """Close the driver connection."""
261:        if hasattr(self.client, 'aclose'):
262:            await self.client.aclose()  # type: ignore[reportUnknownMemberType]
263:        elif hasattr(self.client.connection, 'aclose'):
264:            await self.client.connection.aclose()
265:        elif hasattr(self.client.connection, 'close'):
266:            await self.client.connection.close()
267:
268:    async def delete_all_indexes(self) -> None:
269:        result = await self.execute_query('CALL db.indexes()')
270:        if not result:
271:            return
272:
273:        records, _, _ = result
274:        drop_tasks = []
275:
276:        for record in records:
277:            label = record['label']
278:            entity_type = record['entitytype']
279:
280:            for field_name, index_type in record['types'].items():
281:                if 'RANGE' in index_type:
282:                    drop_tasks.append(self.execute_query(f'DROP INDEX ON :{label}({field_name})'))
283:                elif 'FULLTEXT' in index_type:
284:                    if entity_type == 'NODE':
285:                        drop_tasks.append(
286:                            self.execute_query(
287:                                f'DROP FULLTEXT INDEX FOR (n:{label}) ON (n.{field_name})'
288:                            )
289:                        )
290:                    elif entity_type == 'RELATIONSHIP':
291:                        drop_tasks.append(
292:                            self.execute_query(
293:                                f'DROP FULLTEXT INDEX FOR ()-[e:{label}]-() ON (e.{field_name})'
294:                            )
295:                        )
296:
297:        if drop_tasks:
298:            await asyncio.gather(*drop_tasks)
299:
300:    async def build_indices_and_constraints(self, delete_existing=False):
301:        if delete_existing:
302:            await self.delete_all_indexes()
303:        index_queries = get_range_indices(self.provider) + get_fulltext_indices(self.provider)
304:        for query in index_queries:
305:            await self.execute_query(query)
306:
307:    def clone(self, database: str) -> 'GraphDriver':
308:        """
309:        Returns a shallow copy of this driver with a different default database.
310:        Reuses the same connection (e.g. FalkorDB, Neo4j).
311:        """
312:        if database == self._database:
313:            cloned = self
314:        elif database == self.default_group_id:
315:            cloned = FalkorDriver(falkor_db=self.client)
316:        else:
317:            # Create a new instance of FalkorDriver with the same connection but a different database
318:            cloned = FalkorDriver(falkor_db=self.client, database=database)
319:
320:        return cloned
321:
322:    async def health_check(self) -> None:
323:        """Check FalkorDB connectivity by running a simple query."""
324:        try:
325:            await self.execute_query('MATCH (n) RETURN 1 LIMIT 1')
326:            return None
327:        except Exception as e:
328:            print(f'FalkorDB health check failed: {e}')
329:            raise
330:
331:    @staticmethod
332:    def convert_datetimes_to_strings(obj):
333:        if isinstance(obj, dict):
334:            return {k: FalkorDriver.convert_datetimes_to_strings(v) for k, v in obj.items()}
335:        elif isinstance(obj, list):
336:            return [FalkorDriver.convert_datetimes_to_strings(item) for item in obj]
337:        elif isinstance(obj, tuple):
338:            return tuple(FalkorDriver.convert_datetimes_to_strings(item) for item in obj)
339:        elif isinstance(obj, datetime):
340:            return obj.isoformat()
341:        else:
342:            return obj
343:
344:    def sanitize(self, query: str) -> str:
345:        """
346:        Replace FalkorDB special characters with whitespace.
347:        Based on FalkorDB tokenization rules: ,.<>{}[]"':;!@#$%^&*()-+=~
348:        """
349:        # FalkorDB separator characters that break text into tokens
350:        separator_map = str.maketrans(
351:            {
352:                ',': ' ',
353:                '.': ' ',
354:                '<': ' ',
355:                '>': ' ',
356:                '{': ' ',
357:                '}': ' ',
358:                '[': ' ',
359:                ']': ' ',
360:                '"': ' ',
361:                "'": ' ',
362:                ':': ' ',
363:                ';': ' ',
364:                '!': ' ',
365:                '@': ' ',
366:                '#': ' ',
367:                '$': ' ',
368:                '%': ' ',
369:                '^': ' ',
370:                '&': ' ',
371:                '*': ' ',
372:                '(': ' ',
373:                ')': ' ',
374:                '-': ' ',
375:                '+': ' ',
376:                '=': ' ',
377:                '~': ' ',
378:                '?': ' ',
379:                '|': ' ',
380:                '/': ' ',
381:                '\\': ' ',
382:            }
383:        )
384:        sanitized = query.translate(separator_map)
385:        # Clean up multiple spaces
386:        sanitized = ' '.join(sanitized.split())
387:        return sanitized
388:
389:    def build_fulltext_query(
390:        self, query: str, group_ids: list[str] | None = None, max_query_length: int = 128
391:    ) -> str:
392:        """
393:        Build a fulltext query string for FalkorDB using RedisSearch syntax.
394:        FalkorDB uses RedisSearch-like syntax where:
395:        - Field queries use @ prefix: @field:value
396:        - Multiple values for same field: (@field:value1|value2)
397:        - Text search doesn't need @ prefix for content fields
398:        - AND is implicit with space: (@group_id:value) (text)
399:        - OR uses pipe within parentheses: (@group_id:value1|value2)
400:        """
401:        validate_group_ids(group_ids)
402:
403:        if group_ids is None or len(group_ids) == 0:
404:            group_filter = ''
405:        else:
406:            # Escape group_ids with quotes to prevent RediSearch syntax errors
407:            # with reserved words like "main" or special characters like hyphens
408:            escaped_group_ids = [f'"{gid}"' for gid in group_ids]
409:            group_values = '|'.join(escaped_group_ids)
410:            group_filter = f'(@group_id:{group_values})'
411:
412:        sanitized_query = self.sanitize(query)
413:
414:        # Remove stopwords and empty tokens from the sanitized query
415:        query_words = sanitized_query.split()
416:        filtered_words = [word for word in query_words if word and word.lower() not in STOPWORDS]
417:        sanitized_query = ' | '.join(filtered_words)
418:
419:        # If the query is too long return no query
420:        if len(sanitized_query.split(' ')) + len(group_ids or '') >= max_query_length:
421:            return ''
422:
423:        full_query = group_filter + ' (' + sanitized_query + ')'
424:
425:        return full_query

73:def _validate_api_key(provider_name: str, api_key: str | None, logger) -> str:
74:    """Validate API key is present.
75:
76:    Args:
77:        provider_name: Name of the provider (e.g., 'OpenAI', 'Anthropic')
78:        api_key: The API key to validate
79:        logger: Logger instance for output
80:
81:    Returns:
82:        The validated API key
83:
84:    Raises:
85:        ValueError: If API key is None or empty
86:    """
87:    if not api_key:
88:        raise ValueError(
89:            f'{provider_name} API key is not configured. Please set the appropriate environment variable.'
90:        )
91:
92:    logger.info(f'Creating {provider_name} client')
93:
94:    return api_key

97:class LLMClientFactory:
98:    """Factory for creating LLM clients based on configuration."""
99:
100:    @staticmethod
101:    def create(config: LLMConfig) -> LLMClient:
102:        """Create an LLM client based on the configured provider."""
103:        import logging
104:
105:        logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
106:
107:        provider = config.provider.lower()
108:
109:        match provider:
110:            case 'openai':
111:                if not config.providers.openai:
112:                    raise ValueError('OpenAI provider configuration not found')
113:
114:                api_key = config.providers.openai.api_key
115:                _validate_api_key('OpenAI', api_key, logger)
116:
117:                from graphiti_core.llm_client.config import LLMConfig as CoreLLMConfig
118:
119:                # Use the same model for both main and small model slots
120:                small_model = config.model
121:
122:                llm_config = CoreLLMConfig(
123:                    api_key=api_key,
124:                    model=config.model,
125:                    small_model=small_model,
126:                    temperature=config.temperature,
127:                    max_tokens=config.max_tokens,
128:                )
129:
130:                # Check if this is a reasoning model (o1, o3, gpt-5 family)
131:                reasoning_prefixes = ('o1', 'o3', 'gpt-5')
132:                is_reasoning_model = config.model.startswith(reasoning_prefixes)
133:
134:                # Only pass reasoning/verbosity parameters for reasoning models (gpt-5 family)
135:                if is_reasoning_model:
136:                    return OpenAIClient(config=llm_config, reasoning='minimal', verbosity='low')
137:                else:
138:                    # For non-reasoning models, explicitly pass None to disable these parameters
139:                    return OpenAIClient(config=llm_config, reasoning=None, verbosity=None)
140:
141:            case 'azure_openai':
142:                if not HAS_AZURE_LLM:
143:                    raise ValueError(
144:                        'Azure OpenAI LLM client not available in current graphiti-core version'
145:                    )
146:                if not config.providers.azure_openai:
147:                    raise ValueError('Azure OpenAI provider configuration not found')
148:                azure_config = config.providers.azure_openai
149:
150:                if not azure_config.api_url:
151:                    raise ValueError('Azure OpenAI API URL is required')
152:
153:                # Currently using API key authentication
154:                # TODO: Add Azure AD authentication support for v1 API compatibility
155:                api_key = azure_config.api_key
156:                _validate_api_key('Azure OpenAI', api_key, logger)
157:
158:                # Azure OpenAI should use the standard AsyncOpenAI client with v1 compatibility endpoint
159:                # See: https://github.com/getzep/graphiti README Azure OpenAI section
160:                from openai import AsyncOpenAI
161:
162:                # Ensure the base_url ends with /openai/v1/ for Azure v1 compatibility
163:                base_url = azure_config.api_url
164:                if not base_url.endswith('/'):
165:                    base_url += '/'
166:                if not base_url.endswith('openai/v1/'):
167:                    base_url += 'openai/v1/'
168:
169:                azure_client = AsyncOpenAI(
170:                    base_url=base_url,
171:                    api_key=api_key,
172:                )
173:
174:                # Then create the LLMConfig
175:                from graphiti_core.llm_client.config import LLMConfig as CoreLLMConfig
176:
177:                llm_config = CoreLLMConfig(
178:                    api_key=api_key,
179:                    base_url=base_url,
180:                    model=config.model,
181:                    temperature=config.temperature,
182:                    max_tokens=config.max_tokens,
183:                )
184:
185:                return AzureOpenAILLMClient(
186:                    azure_client=azure_client,
187:                    config=llm_config,
188:                    max_tokens=config.max_tokens,
189:                )
190:
191:            case 'anthropic':
192:                if not HAS_ANTHROPIC:
193:                    raise ValueError(
194:                        'Anthropic client not available in current graphiti-core version'
195:                    )
196:                if not config.providers.anthropic:
197:                    raise ValueError('Anthropic provider configuration not found')
198:
199:                api_key = config.providers.anthropic.api_key
200:                _validate_api_key('Anthropic', api_key, logger)
201:
202:                llm_config = GraphitiLLMConfig(
203:                    api_key=api_key,
204:                    model=config.model,
205:                    temperature=config.temperature,
206:                    max_tokens=config.max_tokens,
207:                )
208:                return AnthropicClient(config=llm_config)
209:
210:            case 'gemini':
211:                if not HAS_GEMINI:
212:                    raise ValueError('Gemini client not available in current graphiti-core version')
213:                if not config.providers.gemini:
214:                    raise ValueError('Gemini provider configuration not found')
215:
216:                api_key = config.providers.gemini.api_key
217:                _validate_api_key('Gemini', api_key, logger)
218:
219:                llm_config = GraphitiLLMConfig(
220:                    api_key=api_key,
221:                    model=config.model,
222:                    temperature=config.temperature,
223:                    max_tokens=config.max_tokens,
224:                )
225:                return GeminiClient(config=llm_config)
226:
227:            case 'groq':
228:                if not HAS_GROQ:
229:                    raise ValueError('Groq client not available in current graphiti-core version')
230:                if not config.providers.groq:
231:                    raise ValueError('Groq provider configuration not found')
232:
233:                api_key = config.providers.groq.api_key
234:                _validate_api_key('Groq', api_key, logger)
235:
236:                llm_config = GraphitiLLMConfig(
237:                    api_key=api_key,
238:                    base_url=config.providers.groq.api_url,
239:                    model=config.model,
240:                    temperature=config.temperature,
241:                    max_tokens=config.max_tokens,
242:                )
243:                return GroqClient(config=llm_config)
244:
245:            case _:
246:                raise ValueError(f'Unsupported LLM provider: {provider}')
247:
248:
249:class EmbedderFactory:
250:    """Factory for creating Embedder clients based on configuration."""
251:
252:    @staticmethod
253:    def create(config: EmbedderConfig) -> EmbedderClient:
254:        """Create an Embedder client based on the configured provider."""
255:        import logging
256:
257:        logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
258:
259:        provider = config.provider.lower()
260:
261:        match provider:
262:            case 'openai':
263:                if not config.providers.openai:
264:                    raise ValueError('OpenAI provider configuration not found')
265:
266:                api_key = config.providers.openai.api_key
267:                _validate_api_key('OpenAI Embedder', api_key, logger)
268:
269:                from graphiti_core.embedder.openai import OpenAIEmbedderConfig
270:
271:                embedder_config = OpenAIEmbedderConfig(
272:                    api_key=api_key,
273:                    embedding_model=config.model,
274:                    base_url=config.providers.openai.api_url,  # Support custom endpoints like Ollama
275:                    embedding_dim=config.dimensions,  # Support custom embedding dimensions
276:                )
277:                return OpenAIEmbedder(config=embedder_config)
278:
279:            case 'azure_openai':
280:                if not HAS_AZURE_EMBEDDER:
281:                    raise ValueError(
282:                        'Azure OpenAI embedder not available in current graphiti-core version'
283:                    )
284:                if not config.providers.azure_openai:
285:                    raise ValueError('Azure OpenAI provider configuration not found')
286:                azure_config = config.providers.azure_openai
287:
288:                if not azure_config.api_url:
289:                    raise ValueError('Azure OpenAI API URL is required')
290:
291:                # Currently using API key authentication
292:                # TODO: Add Azure AD authentication support for v1 API compatibility
293:                api_key = azure_config.api_key
294:                _validate_api_key('Azure OpenAI Embedder', api_key, logger)
295:
296:                # Azure OpenAI should use the standard AsyncOpenAI client with v1 compatibility endpoint
297:                # See: https://github.com/getzep/graphiti README Azure OpenAI section
298:                from openai import AsyncOpenAI
299:
300:                # Ensure the base_url ends with /openai/v1/ for Azure v1 compatibility
301:                base_url = azure_config.api_url
302:                if not base_url.endswith('/'):
303:                    base_url += '/'
304:                if not base_url.endswith('openai/v1/'):
305:                    base_url += 'openai/v1/'
306:
307:                azure_client = AsyncOpenAI(
308:                    base_url=base_url,
309:                    api_key=api_key,
310:                )
311:
312:                return AzureOpenAIEmbedderClient(
313:                    azure_client=azure_client,
314:                    model=config.model or 'text-embedding-3-small',
315:                )
316:
317:            case 'gemini':
318:                if not HAS_GEMINI_EMBEDDER:
319:                    raise ValueError(
320:                        'Gemini embedder not available in current graphiti-core version'
321:                    )
322:                if not config.providers.gemini:
323:                    raise ValueError('Gemini provider configuration not found')
324:
325:                api_key = config.providers.gemini.api_key
326:                _validate_api_key('Gemini Embedder', api_key, logger)
327:
328:                from graphiti_core.embedder.gemini import GeminiEmbedderConfig
329:
330:                gemini_config = GeminiEmbedderConfig(
331:                    api_key=api_key,
332:                    embedding_model=config.model or 'models/text-embedding-004',
333:                    embedding_dim=config.dimensions or 768,
334:                )
335:                return GeminiEmbedder(config=gemini_config)
336:
337:            case 'voyage':
338:                if not HAS_VOYAGE_EMBEDDER:
339:                    raise ValueError(
340:                        'Voyage embedder not available in current graphiti-core version'
341:                    )
342:                if not config.providers.voyage:
343:                    raise ValueError('Voyage provider configuration not found')
344:
345:                api_key = config.providers.voyage.api_key
346:                _validate_api_key('Voyage Embedder', api_key, logger)
347:
348:                from graphiti_core.embedder.voyage import VoyageAIEmbedderConfig
349:
350:                voyage_config = VoyageAIEmbedderConfig(
351:                    api_key=api_key,
352:                    embedding_model=config.model or 'voyage-3',
353:                    embedding_dim=config.dimensions or 1024,
354:                )
355:                return VoyageAIEmbedder(config=voyage_config)
356:
357:            case _:
358:                raise ValueError(f'Unsupported Embedder provider: {provider}')
359:
360:
361:class DatabaseDriverFactory:
362:    """Factory for creating Database drivers based on configuration.
363:
364:    Note: This returns configuration dictionaries that can be passed to Graphiti(),
365:    not driver instances directly, as the drivers require complex initialization.
366:    """
367:
368:    @staticmethod
369:    def create_config(config: DatabaseConfig) -> dict:
370:        """Create database configuration dictionary based on the configured provider."""
371:        provider = config.provider.lower()
372:
373:        match provider:
374:            case 'neo4j':
375:                # Use Neo4j config if provided, otherwise use defaults
376:                if config.providers.neo4j:
377:                    neo4j_config = config.providers.neo4j
378:                else:
379:                    # Create default Neo4j configuration
380:                    from config.schema import Neo4jProviderConfig
381:
382:                    neo4j_config = Neo4jProviderConfig()
383:
384:                # Check for environment variable overrides (for CI/CD compatibility)
385:                import os
386:
387:                uri = os.environ.get('NEO4J_URI', neo4j_config.uri)
388:                username = os.environ.get('NEO4J_USER', neo4j_config.username)
389:                password = os.environ.get('NEO4J_PASSWORD', neo4j_config.password)
390:
391:                return {
392:                    'uri': uri,
393:                    'user': username,
394:                    'password': password,
395:                    # Note: database and use_parallel_runtime would need to be passed
396:                    # to the driver after initialization if supported
397:                }
398:
399:            case 'falkordb':
400:                if not HAS_FALKOR:
401:                    raise ValueError(
402:                        'FalkorDB driver not available in current graphiti-core version'
403:                    )
404:
405:                # Use FalkorDB config if provided, otherwise use defaults
406:                if config.providers.falkordb:
407:                    falkor_config = config.providers.falkordb
408:                else:
409:                    # Create default FalkorDB configuration
410:                    from config.schema import FalkorDBProviderConfig
411:
412:                    falkor_config = FalkorDBProviderConfig()
413:
414:                # Check for environment variable overrides (for CI/CD compatibility)
415:                import os
416:                from urllib.parse import urlparse
417:
418:                uri = os.environ.get('FALKORDB_URI', falkor_config.uri)
419:                password = os.environ.get('FALKORDB_PASSWORD', falkor_config.password)
420:
421:                # Parse the URI to extract host and port
422:                parsed = urlparse(uri)
423:                host = parsed.hostname or 'localhost'
424:                port = parsed.port or 6379
425:
426:                return {
427:                    'driver': 'falkordb',
428:                    'host': host,
429:                    'port': port,
430:                    'password': password,
431:                    'database': falkor_config.database,
432:                }
433:
434:            case _:
435:                raise ValueError(f'Unsupported Database provider: {provider}')

30:> [!NOTE]
31:> **We're Hiring!** Build context graphs that power reliable, personalized, fast production AI agents.
32:> Come build with us — we're hiring Engineers and Developer Relations folks. [View open roles](https://www.getzep.com/careers/).
