440:    # --- Episode search ---
441:
442:    async def episode_fulltext_search(
443:        self,
444:        executor: QueryExecutor,
445:        query: str,
446:        search_filter: SearchFilters,  # noqa: ARG002
447:        group_ids: list[str] | None = None,
448:        limit: int = 10,
449:    ) -> list[EpisodicNode]:
450:        fuzzy_query = _build_falkor_fulltext_query(query, group_ids)
451:        if fuzzy_query == '':
452:            return []
453:
454:        filter_params: dict[str, Any] = {}
455:        group_filter_query = ''
456:        if group_ids is not None:
457:            group_filter_query += '\nAND e.group_id IN $group_ids'
458:            filter_params['group_ids'] = group_ids
459:
460:        cypher = (
461:            get_nodes_query(
462:                'episode_content', '$query', limit=limit, provider=GraphProvider.FALKORDB
463:            )
464:            + """
465:            YIELD node AS episode, score
466:            MATCH (e:Episodic)
467:            WHERE e.uuid = episode.uuid
468:            """
469:            + group_filter_query
470:            + """
471:            RETURN
472:            """
473:            + EPISODIC_NODE_RETURN
474:            + """
475:            ORDER BY score DESC
476:            LIMIT $limit
477:            """
478:        )
479:
480:        records, _, _ = await executor.execute_query(
481:            cypher, query=fuzzy_query, limit=limit, **filter_params
482:        )
483:
484:        return [episodic_node_from_record(r) for r in records]

486:    # --- Community search ---
487:
488:    async def community_fulltext_search(
489:        self,
490:        executor: QueryExecutor,
491:        query: str,
492:        group_ids: list[str] | None = None,
493:        limit: int = 10,
494:    ) -> list[CommunityNode]:
495:        fuzzy_query = _build_falkor_fulltext_query(query, group_ids)
496:        if fuzzy_query == '':
497:            return []
498:
499:        filter_params: dict[str, Any] = {}
500:        group_filter_query = ''
501:        if group_ids is not None:
502:            group_filter_query = 'WHERE c.group_id IN $group_ids'
503:            filter_params['group_ids'] = group_ids
504:
505:        cypher = (
506:            get_nodes_query(
507:                'community_name', '$query', limit=limit, provider=GraphProvider.FALKORDB
508:            )
509:            + """
510:            YIELD node AS c, score
511:            WITH c, score
512:            """
513:            + group_filter_query
514:            + """
515:            RETURN
516:            """
517:            + COMMUNITY_NODE_RETURN
518:            + """
519:            ORDER BY score DESC
520:            LIMIT $limit
521:            """
522:        )
523:
524:        records, _, _ = await executor.execute_query(
525:            cypher, query=fuzzy_query, limit=limit, **filter_params
526:        )
527:
528:        return [community_node_from_record(r) for r in records]
529:
530:    async def community_similarity_search(
531:        self,
532:        executor: QueryExecutor,
533:        search_vector: list[float],
534:        group_ids: list[str] | None = None,
535:        limit: int = 10,
536:        min_score: float = 0.6,
537:    ) -> list[CommunityNode]:
538:        query_params: dict[str, Any] = {}
539:
540:        group_filter_query = ''
541:        if group_ids is not None:
542:            group_filter_query += ' WHERE c.group_id IN $group_ids'
543:            query_params['group_ids'] = group_ids
544:
545:        cypher = (
546:            'MATCH (c:Community)'
547:            + group_filter_query
548:            + """
549:            WITH c,
550:            """
551:            + get_vector_cosine_func_query(
552:                'c.name_embedding', '$search_vector', GraphProvider.FALKORDB
553:            )
554:            + """ AS score
555:            WHERE score > $min_score
556:            RETURN
557:            """
558:            + COMMUNITY_NODE_RETURN
559:            + """
560:            ORDER BY score DESC
561:            LIMIT $limit
562:            """
563:        )
564:
565:        records, _, _ = await executor.execute_query(
566:            cypher,
567:            search_vector=search_vector,
568:            limit=limit,
569:            min_score=min_score,
570:            **query_params,
571:        )
572:
573:        return [community_node_from_record(r) for r in records]
574:
575:    # --- Rerankers ---
576:
577:    async def node_distance_reranker(
578:        self,
579:        executor: QueryExecutor,
580:        node_uuids: list[str],
581:        center_node_uuid: str,
582:        min_score: float = 0,
583:    ) -> list[EntityNode]:
584:        filtered_uuids = [u for u in node_uuids if u != center_node_uuid]
585:        scores: dict[str, float] = {center_node_uuid: 0.0}
586:
587:        cypher = """
588:        UNWIND $node_uuids AS node_uuid
589:        MATCH (center:Entity {uuid: $center_uuid})-[:RELATES_TO]-(n:Entity {uuid: node_uuid})
590:        RETURN 1 AS score, node_uuid AS uuid
591:        """
592:
593:        results, _, _ = await executor.execute_query(
594:            cypher,
595:            node_uuids=filtered_uuids,
596:            center_uuid=center_node_uuid,
597:        )
598:
599:        for result in results:
600:            scores[result['uuid']] = result['score']
601:
602:        for uuid in filtered_uuids:
603:            if uuid not in scores:
604:                scores[uuid] = float('inf')
605:
606:        filtered_uuids.sort(key=lambda cur_uuid: scores[cur_uuid])
607:
608:        if center_node_uuid in node_uuids:
609:            scores[center_node_uuid] = 0.1
610:            filtered_uuids = [center_node_uuid] + filtered_uuids
611:
612:        reranked_uuids = [u for u in filtered_uuids if (1 / scores[u]) >= min_score]
613:
614:        if not reranked_uuids:
615:            return []
616:
617:        get_query = """
618:            MATCH (n:Entity)
619:            WHERE n.uuid IN $uuids
620:            RETURN
621:            """ + get_entity_node_return_query(GraphProvider.FALKORDB)
622:
623:        records, _, _ = await executor.execute_query(get_query, uuids=reranked_uuids)
624:
625:        node_map = {r['uuid']: entity_node_from_record(r) for r in records}
626:        return [node_map[u] for u in reranked_uuids if u in node_map]
627:
628:    async def episode_mentions_reranker(
629:        self,
630:        executor: QueryExecutor,
631:        node_uuids: list[str],
632:        min_score: float = 0,
633:    ) -> list[EntityNode]:
634:        if not node_uuids:
635:            return []
636:
637:        scores: dict[str, float] = {}
638:
639:        results, _, _ = await executor.execute_query(
640:            """
641:            UNWIND $node_uuids AS node_uuid
642:            MATCH (episode:Episodic)-[r:MENTIONS]->(n:Entity {uuid: node_uuid})
643:            RETURN count(*) AS score, n.uuid AS uuid
644:            """,
645:            node_uuids=node_uuids,
646:        )
647:
648:        for result in results:
649:            scores[result['uuid']] = result['score']
650:
651:        for uuid in node_uuids:
652:            if uuid not in scores:
653:                scores[uuid] = float('inf')
654:
655:        sorted_uuids = list(node_uuids)
656:        sorted_uuids.sort(key=lambda cur_uuid: scores[cur_uuid])
657:
658:        reranked_uuids = [u for u in sorted_uuids if scores[u] >= min_score]
659:
660:        if not reranked_uuids:
661:            return []
662:
663:        get_query = """
664:            MATCH (n:Entity)
665:            WHERE n.uuid IN $uuids
666:            RETURN
667:            """ + get_entity_node_return_query(GraphProvider.FALKORDB)
668:
669:        records, _, _ = await executor.execute_query(get_query, uuids=reranked_uuids)
670:
671:        node_map = {r['uuid']: entity_node_from_record(r) for r in records}
672:        return [node_map[u] for u in reranked_uuids if u in node_map]

51:def build_episodic_edges(
52:    entity_nodes: list[EntityNode],
53:    episode_uuid: str | list[str],
54:    created_at: datetime,
55:    node_episode_index_map: dict[str, list[int]] | None = None,
56:) -> list[EpisodicEdge]:
57:    """Build episodic (MENTIONED_IN) edges between entity nodes and episodes.
58:
59:    Parameters
60:    ----------
61:    entity_nodes : list[EntityNode]
62:        Nodes to connect to episodes.
63:    episode_uuid : str | list[str]
64:        A single episode UUID or a list of episode UUIDs.
65:    created_at : datetime
66:        Timestamp for the edges.
67:    node_episode_index_map : dict[str, list[int]] | None
68:        Optional mapping from node UUID to 0-indexed episode positions.
69:        When provided with a list of episode_uuids, each node is connected
70:        only to its attributed episodes. When None, every node is connected
71:        to all episodes.
72:    """
73:    episode_uuids = [episode_uuid] if isinstance(episode_uuid, str) else episode_uuid
74:
75:    episodic_edges: list[EpisodicEdge] = []
76:    for node in entity_nodes:
77:        if node_episode_index_map and node.uuid in node_episode_index_map:
78:            indices = node_episode_index_map[node.uuid]
79:        else:
80:            indices = list(range(len(episode_uuids)))
81:
82:        for idx in indices:
83:            if 0 <= idx < len(episode_uuids):
84:                episodic_edges.append(
85:                    EpisodicEdge(
86:                        source_node_uuid=episode_uuids[idx],
87:                        target_node_uuid=node.uuid,
88:                        created_at=created_at,
89:                        group_id=node.group_id,
90:                    )
91:                )
92:
93:    logger.debug(f'Built {len(episodic_edges)} episodic edges')
94:
95:    return episodic_edges

98:def build_community_edges(
99:    entity_nodes: list[EntityNode],
100:    community_node: CommunityNode,
101:    created_at: datetime,
102:) -> list[CommunityEdge]:
103:    edges: list[CommunityEdge] = [
104:        CommunityEdge(
105:            source_node_uuid=community_node.uuid,
106:            target_node_uuid=node.uuid,
107:            created_at=created_at,
108:            group_id=community_node.group_id,
109:        )
110:        for node in entity_nodes
111:    ]
112:
113:    return edges

116:async def extract_edges(
117:    clients: GraphitiClients,
118:    episode: EpisodicNode | list[EpisodicNode],
119:    nodes: list[EntityNode],
120:    previous_episodes: list[EpisodicNode],
121:    edge_type_map: dict[tuple[str, str], list[str]],
122:    group_id: str = '',
123:    edge_types: dict[str, type[BaseModel]] | None = None,
124:    custom_extraction_instructions: str | None = None,
125:) -> list[EntityEdge]:
126:    """Extract edges from one or more episodes.
127:
128:    Parameters
129:    ----------
130:    episode : EpisodicNode | list[EpisodicNode]
131:        A single episode or a list of episodes to extract edges from.
132:        When a list is provided, their contents are concatenated for extraction
133:        and edges are linked to all episode UUIDs.
134:    """
135:    episodes = episode if isinstance(episode, list) else [episode]
136:    primary_episode = episodes[0]
137:
138:    start = time()
139:
140:    extract_edges_max_tokens = 16384
141:    llm_client = clients.llm_client

143:    # Build mapping from edge type name to list of valid signatures
144:    edge_type_signatures_map: dict[str, list[tuple[str, str]]] = {}
145:    for signature, edge_type_names in edge_type_map.items():
146:        for edge_type in edge_type_names:
147:            if edge_type not in edge_type_signatures_map:
148:                edge_type_signatures_map[edge_type] = []
149:            edge_type_signatures_map[edge_type].append(signature)
150:
151:    edge_types_context = (
152:        [
153:            {
154:                'fact_type_name': type_name,
155:                'fact_type_signatures': edge_type_signatures_map.get(
156:                    type_name, [('Entity', 'Entity')]
157:                ),
158:                'fact_type_description': type_model.__doc__,
159:            }
160:            for type_name, type_model in edge_types.items()
161:        ]
162:        if edge_types is not None
163:        else []
164:    )
165:
166:    # Build name-to-node mapping for validation
167:    name_to_node: dict[str, EntityNode] = {node.name: node for node in nodes}
168:
169:    # Build episode attribution instructions for multi-episode extraction
170:    episode_attribution = ''
171:    if len(episodes) > 1:
172:        episode_attribution = (
173:            '\n8. **Episode Attribution**: The CURRENT_MESSAGE contains multiple episodes labeled '
174:            '[Episode 0], [Episode 1], etc. Each episode header includes a timestamp indicating '
175:            'when that episode occurred. Use the per-episode timestamp to resolve relative time '
176:            'mentions within each episode rather than relying solely on REFERENCE_TIME. '
177:            'For each extracted fact, set `episode_indices` '
178:            'to the 0-based list of episode numbers that the fact was derived from. '
179:            'A fact sourced from Episodes 0 and 1 should have `episode_indices: [0, 1]`.'
180:        )
181:
182:    # Prepare context for LLM
183:    # Use the latest episode's timestamp as the primary reference time
184:    latest_episode = max(episodes, key=lambda ep: ep.valid_at)
185:    context = {
186:        'episode_content': concatenate_episodes(episodes),
187:        'nodes': [{'name': node.name, 'entity_types': node.labels} for node in nodes],
188:        'previous_episodes': [
189:            {
190:                'content': ep.content,
191:                'timestamp': ep.valid_at.isoformat() if ep.valid_at else None,
192:            }
193:            for ep in previous_episodes
194:        ],
195:        'reference_time': latest_episode.valid_at,
196:        'edge_types': edge_types_context,
197:        'custom_extraction_instructions': (custom_extraction_instructions or '')
198:        + episode_attribution,
199:    }
200:
201:    llm_response = await llm_client.generate_response(
202:        prompt_library.extract_edges.edge(context),
203:        response_model=ExtractedEdges,
204:        max_tokens=extract_edges_max_tokens,
205:        group_id=group_id or primary_episode.group_id,
206:        prompt_name='extract_edges.edge',
207:    )
208:    all_edges_data = ExtractedEdges(**llm_response).edges
209:
210:    # Validate entity names
211:    edges_data: list[ExtractedEdge] = []
212:    for edge_data in all_edges_data:
213:        source_name = edge_data.source_entity_name
214:        target_name = edge_data.target_entity_name
215:
216:        # Validate LLM-returned names exist in the nodes list
217:        if source_name not in name_to_node:
218:            logger.warning(
219:                'Source entity not found in nodes for edge relation: %s',
220:                edge_data.relation_type,
221:            )
222:            continue
223:
224:        if target_name not in name_to_node:
225:            logger.warning(
226:                'Target entity not found in nodes for edge relation: %s',
227:                edge_data.relation_type,
228:            )
229:            continue
230:
231:        # Drop self-edges where source and target resolve to the same node
232:        source_node = name_to_node[source_name]
233:        target_node = name_to_node[target_name]
234:        if source_node.uuid == target_node.uuid:
235:            logger.info(
236:                'Dropping self-edge for node %s (source and target resolve to same node)',
237:                source_node.uuid,
238:            )
239:            continue
240:
241:        edges_data.append(edge_data)
242:
243:    end = time()
244:    logger.debug(f'Extracted {len(edges_data)} new edges in {(end - start) * 1000:.0f} ms')
245:
246:    if len(edges_data) == 0:
247:        return []
248:
249:    # Convert the extracted data into EntityEdge objects
250:    edges = []
251:    for edge_data in edges_data:
252:        # Validate Edge Date information
253:        valid_at = edge_data.valid_at
254:        invalid_at = edge_data.invalid_at
255:        valid_at_datetime = None
256:        invalid_at_datetime = None
257:
258:        # Filter out empty edges
259:        if not edge_data.fact.strip():
260:            continue
261:
262:        # Names already validated above
263:        source_node = name_to_node.get(edge_data.source_entity_name)
264:        target_node = name_to_node.get(edge_data.target_entity_name)
265:
266:        if source_node is None or target_node is None:
267:            logger.warning('Could not find source or target node for extracted edge')
268:            continue
269:
270:        source_node_uuid = source_node.uuid
271:        target_node_uuid = target_node.uuid
272:
273:        if valid_at:
274:            try:
275:                valid_at_datetime = ensure_utc(
276:                    datetime.fromisoformat(valid_at.replace('Z', '+00:00'))
277:                )
278:            except ValueError:
279:                logger.warning('Error parsing valid_at date, skipping')
280:
281:        if invalid_at:
282:            try:
283:                invalid_at_datetime = ensure_utc(
284:                    datetime.fromisoformat(invalid_at.replace('Z', '+00:00'))
285:                )
286:            except ValueError as e:
287:                logger.warning(f'WARNING: Error parsing invalid_at date: {e}. Input: {invalid_at}')
288:
289:        # Map episode_indices (0-indexed) to episode UUIDs.
290:        # Clamp indices to valid range and fall back to all episodes if empty.
291:        edge_episode_uuids = []
292:        for idx in edge_data.episode_indices:
293:            if 0 <= idx < len(episodes):
294:                edge_episode_uuids.append(episodes[idx].uuid)
295:        if not edge_episode_uuids:
296:            edge_episode_uuids = [ep.uuid for ep in episodes]
297:
298:        edge = EntityEdge(
299:            source_node_uuid=source_node_uuid,
300:            target_node_uuid=target_node_uuid,
301:            name=edge_data.relation_type,
302:            group_id=group_id or primary_episode.group_id,
303:            fact=edge_data.fact,
304:            episodes=edge_episode_uuids,
305:            created_at=utc_now(),
306:            valid_at=valid_at_datetime,
307:            invalid_at=invalid_at_datetime,
308:            reference_time=(
309:                episodes[edge_data.episode_indices[0]].valid_at
310:                if edge_data.episode_indices and 0 <= edge_data.episode_indices[0] < len(episodes)
311:                else primary_episode.valid_at
312:            ),
313:        )
314:        edges.append(edge)
315:        logger.debug(
316:            f'Created new edge {edge.uuid} from {edge.source_node_uuid} to {edge.target_node_uuid}'
317:        )
318:
319:    logger.debug(f'Extracted edges: {[e.uuid for e in edges]}')
320:
321:    return edges
322:
323:
324:async def resolve_extracted_edges(
325:    clients: GraphitiClients,
326:    extracted_edges: list[EntityEdge],
327:    episode: EpisodicNode,
328:    entities: list[EntityNode],
329:    edge_types: dict[str, type[BaseModel]],
330:    edge_type_map: dict[tuple[str, str], list[str]],
331:    existing_edges_override: list[EntityEdge] | None = None,
332:) -> tuple[list[EntityEdge], list[EntityEdge], list[EntityEdge]]:
333:    """Resolve extracted edges against existing graph context.
334:
335:    Returns
336:    -------
337:    tuple[list[EntityEdge], list[EntityEdge], list[EntityEdge]]
338:        A tuple of (resolved_edges, invalidated_edges, new_edges) where:
339:        - resolved_edges: All edges after resolution (may include existing edges if duplicates found)
340:        - invalidated_edges: Edges that were invalidated/contradicted by new information
341:        - new_edges: Only edges that are new to the graph (not duplicates of existing edges)
342:    """
343:    # Fast path: deduplicate exact matches within the extracted edges before parallel processing
344:    seen: dict[tuple[str, str, str], EntityEdge] = {}
345:    deduplicated_edges: list[EntityEdge] = []
346:
347:    for edge in extracted_edges:
348:        key = (
349:            edge.source_node_uuid,
350:            edge.target_node_uuid,
351:            _normalize_string_exact(edge.fact),
352:        )
353:        if key not in seen:
354:            seen[key] = edge
355:            deduplicated_edges.append(edge)
356:
357:    extracted_edges = deduplicated_edges
358:
359:    driver = clients.driver
360:    llm_client = clients.llm_client
361:    embedder = clients.embedder
362:    await create_entity_edge_embeddings(embedder, extracted_edges)
363:
364:    valid_edges_list: list[list[EntityEdge]] = await semaphore_gather(
365:        *[
366:            EntityEdge.get_between_nodes(driver, edge.source_node_uuid, edge.target_node_uuid)
367:            for edge in extracted_edges
368:        ]
369:    )
370:
371:    # Merge override edges (e.g. from the recent Redis dedup cache) into
372:    # the per-extracted-edge candidate lists so that recently resolved edges
373:    # that are not yet visible in the graph-service indexes are still
374:    # considered during deduplication.
375:    if existing_edges_override:
376:        override_by_pair: dict[tuple[str, str], list[EntityEdge]] = {}
377:        for oe in existing_edges_override:
378:            key = (oe.source_node_uuid, oe.target_node_uuid)
379:            override_by_pair.setdefault(key, []).append(oe)
380:
381:        for i, extracted_edge in enumerate(extracted_edges):
382:            pair_key = (extracted_edge.source_node_uuid, extracted_edge.target_node_uuid)
383:            overrides = override_by_pair.get(pair_key, [])
384:            if overrides:
385:                existing_uuids = {e.uuid for e in valid_edges_list[i]}
386:                for oe in overrides:
387:                    if oe.uuid not in existing_uuids:
388:                        valid_edges_list[i].append(oe)
389:                        existing_uuids.add(oe.uuid)
390:
391:    related_edges_results: list[SearchResults] = await semaphore_gather(
392:        *[
393:            search(
394:                clients,
395:                extracted_edge.fact,
396:                group_ids=[extracted_edge.group_id],
397:                config=EDGE_HYBRID_SEARCH_RRF,
398:                search_filter=SearchFilters(edge_uuids=[edge.uuid for edge in valid_edges]),
399:            )
400:            for extracted_edge, valid_edges in zip(extracted_edges, valid_edges_list, strict=True)
401:        ]
402:    )
403:
404:    related_edges_lists: list[list[EntityEdge]] = [result.edges for result in related_edges_results]
405:
406:    edge_invalidation_candidate_results: list[SearchResults] = await semaphore_gather(
407:        *[
408:            search(
409:                clients,
410:                extracted_edge.fact,
411:                group_ids=[extracted_edge.group_id],
412:                config=EDGE_HYBRID_SEARCH_RRF,
413:                search_filter=SearchFilters(),
414:            )
415:            for extracted_edge in extracted_edges
416:        ]
417:    )
418:
419:    # Remove duplicates: if an edge appears in both duplicate candidates and invalidation candidates,
420:    # keep it only in duplicate candidates
421:    edge_invalidation_candidates: list[list[EntityEdge]] = []
422:    for related_edges, invalidation_result in zip(
423:        related_edges_lists, edge_invalidation_candidate_results, strict=True
424:    ):
425:        related_uuids = {edge.uuid for edge in related_edges}
426:        deduplicated = [
427:            edge for edge in invalidation_result.edges if edge.uuid not in related_uuids
428:        ]
429:        edge_invalidation_candidates.append(deduplicated)
430:
431:    logger.debug(
432:        f'Related edges: {[e.uuid for edges_lst in related_edges_lists for e in edges_lst]}'
433:    )
434:
435:    # Build entity hash table
436:    uuid_entity_map: dict[str, EntityNode] = {entity.uuid: entity for entity in entities}
437:
438:    # Collect all node UUIDs referenced by edges that are not in the entities list
439:    referenced_node_uuids = set()
440:    for extracted_edge in extracted_edges:
441:        if extracted_edge.source_node_uuid not in uuid_entity_map:
442:            referenced_node_uuids.add(extracted_edge.source_node_uuid)
443:        if extracted_edge.target_node_uuid not in uuid_entity_map:
444:            referenced_node_uuids.add(extracted_edge.target_node_uuid)
445:
446:    # Fetch missing nodes from the database
447:    if referenced_node_uuids:
448:        # Pass group_id so graph-service implementations can scope the lookup
449:        edge_group_id = extracted_edges[0].group_id
450:        missing_nodes = await EntityNode.get_by_uuids(
451:            driver, list(referenced_node_uuids), group_id=edge_group_id
452:        )
453:        for node in missing_nodes:
454:            uuid_entity_map[node.uuid] = node
455:
456:    # Determine which edge types are relevant for each edge based on node signatures.
457:    # `edge_types_lst` stores the subset of custom edge definitions whose
458:    # node signature matches each extracted edge.
459:    edge_types_lst: list[dict[str, type[BaseModel]]] = []
460:    for extracted_edge in extracted_edges:
461:        source_node = uuid_entity_map.get(extracted_edge.source_node_uuid)
462:        target_node = uuid_entity_map.get(extracted_edge.target_node_uuid)
463:        source_node_labels = (
464:            source_node.labels + ['Entity'] if source_node is not None else ['Entity']
465:        )
466:        target_node_labels = (
467:            target_node.labels + ['Entity'] if target_node is not None else ['Entity']
468:        )
469:        label_tuples = [
470:            (source_label, target_label)
471:            for source_label in source_node_labels
472:            for target_label in target_node_labels
473:        ]
474:
475:        extracted_edge_types = {}
476:        for label_tuple in label_tuples:
477:            type_names = edge_type_map.get(label_tuple, [])
478:            for type_name in type_names:
479:                type_model = edge_types.get(type_name)
480:                if type_model is None:
481:                    continue
482:
483:                extracted_edge_types[type_name] = type_model
484:
485:        edge_types_lst.append(extracted_edge_types)
486:
487:    # resolve edges with related edges in the graph and find invalidation candidates
488:    results: list[tuple[EntityEdge, list[EntityEdge], list[EntityEdge]]] = list(
489:        await semaphore_gather(
490:            *[
491:                resolve_extracted_edge(
492:                    llm_client,
493:                    extracted_edge,
494:                    related_edges,
495:                    existing_edges,
496:                    episode,
497:                    extracted_edge_types,
498:                )
499:                for extracted_edge, related_edges, existing_edges, extracted_edge_types in zip(
500:                    extracted_edges,
501:                    related_edges_lists,
502:                    edge_invalidation_candidates,
503:                    edge_types_lst,
504:                    strict=True,
505:                )
506:            ]
507:        )
508:    )
509:
510:    resolved_edges: list[EntityEdge] = []
511:    invalidated_edges: list[EntityEdge] = []
512:    new_edges: list[EntityEdge] = []
513:    for extracted_edge, result in zip(extracted_edges, results, strict=True):
514:        resolved_edge = result[0]
515:        invalidated_edge_chunk = result[1]
516:        # result[2] is duplicate_edges list
517:
518:        resolved_edges.append(resolved_edge)
519:        invalidated_edges.extend(invalidated_edge_chunk)
520:
521:        # Track edges that are new (not duplicates of existing edges)
522:        # An edge is new if the resolved edge UUID matches the extracted edge UUID
523:        if resolved_edge.uuid == extracted_edge.uuid:
524:            new_edges.append(resolved_edge)
525:
526:    logger.debug(f'Resolved edges: {[e.uuid for e in resolved_edges]}')
527:    logger.debug(f'New edges (non-duplicates): {[e.uuid for e in new_edges]}')
528:
529:    await semaphore_gather(
530:        create_entity_edge_embeddings(embedder, resolved_edges),
531:        create_entity_edge_embeddings(embedder, invalidated_edges),
532:    )
533:
534:    return resolved_edges, invalidated_edges, new_edges
535:
536:
537:def resolve_edge_contradictions(
538:    resolved_edge: EntityEdge, invalidation_candidates: list[EntityEdge]
539:) -> list[EntityEdge]:
540:    if len(invalidation_candidates) == 0:
541:        return []
542:
543:    # Determine which contradictory edges need to be expired
544:    invalidated_edges: list[EntityEdge] = []
545:    for edge in invalidation_candidates:
546:        # (Edge invalid before new edge becomes valid) or (new edge invalid before edge becomes valid)
547:        edge_invalid_at_utc = ensure_utc(edge.invalid_at)
548:        resolved_edge_valid_at_utc = ensure_utc(resolved_edge.valid_at)
549:        edge_valid_at_utc = ensure_utc(edge.valid_at)
550:        resolved_edge_invalid_at_utc = ensure_utc(resolved_edge.invalid_at)
551:
552:        if (
553:            edge_invalid_at_utc is not None
554:            and resolved_edge_valid_at_utc is not None
555:            and edge_invalid_at_utc <= resolved_edge_valid_at_utc
556:        ) or (
557:            edge_valid_at_utc is not None
558:            and resolved_edge_invalid_at_utc is not None
559:            and resolved_edge_invalid_at_utc <= edge_valid_at_utc
560:        ):
561:            continue
562:        # New edge invalidates edge
563:        elif (
564:            edge_valid_at_utc is not None
565:            and resolved_edge_valid_at_utc is not None
566:            and edge_valid_at_utc < resolved_edge_valid_at_utc
567:        ):
568:            edge.invalid_at = resolved_edge.valid_at
569:            edge.expired_at = edge.expired_at if edge.expired_at is not None else utc_now()
570:            invalidated_edges.append(edge)
571:
572:    return invalidated_edges
573:
574:
575:async def _extract_edge_timestamps(
576:    llm_client: LLMClient,
577:    edge: EntityEdge,
578:    episode: EpisodicNode | None,
579:) -> None:
580:    """Extract valid_at / invalid_at timestamps for an edge via a lightweight LLM call.
581:
582:    Modifies the edge in place. Skips if the edge already has timestamps set
583:    (e.g., from the extraction prompt in the separate-extraction path) or if
584:    no reference time is available.
585:    """
586:    if edge.valid_at is not None or edge.invalid_at is not None:
587:        return
588:
589:    if episode is None or episode.valid_at is None:
590:        return
591:
592:    context = {
593:        'fact': edge.fact,
594:        'reference_time': episode.valid_at.isoformat(),
595:    }
596:    try:
597:        llm_response = await llm_client.generate_response(
598:            prompt_library.extract_edges.extract_timestamps(context),
599:            response_model=EdgeTimestamps,
600:            model_size=ModelSize.small,
601:            prompt_name='extract_edges.extract_timestamps',
602:        )
603:        timestamps = EdgeTimestamps(**llm_response)
604:        if timestamps.valid_at:
605:            try:
606:                edge.valid_at = ensure_utc(
607:                    datetime.fromisoformat(timestamps.valid_at.replace('Z', '+00:00'))
608:                )
609:            except ValueError:
610:                logger.debug(f'Error parsing valid_at: {timestamps.valid_at}')
611:        if timestamps.invalid_at:
612:            try:
613:                edge.invalid_at = ensure_utc(
614:                    datetime.fromisoformat(timestamps.invalid_at.replace('Z', '+00:00'))
615:                )
616:            except ValueError:
617:                logger.debug(f'Error parsing invalid_at: {timestamps.invalid_at}')
618:    except Exception:
619:        logger.warning('Failed to extract timestamps for edge %s', edge.uuid, exc_info=True)
620:
621:
622:async def resolve_extracted_edge(
623:    llm_client: LLMClient,
624:    extracted_edge: EntityEdge,
625:    related_edges: list[EntityEdge],
626:    existing_edges: list[EntityEdge],
627:    episode: EpisodicNode,
628:    edge_type_candidates: dict[str, type[BaseModel]] | None = None,
629:) -> tuple[EntityEdge, list[EntityEdge], list[EntityEdge]]:
630:    """Resolve an extracted edge against existing graph context.
631:
632:    Parameters
633:    ----------
634:    llm_client : LLMClient
635:        Client used to invoke the LLM for deduplication and attribute extraction.
636:    extracted_edge : EntityEdge
637:        Newly extracted edge whose canonical representation is being resolved.
638:    related_edges : list[EntityEdge]
639:        Candidate edges with identical endpoints used for duplicate detection.
640:    existing_edges : list[EntityEdge]
641:        Broader set of edges evaluated for contradiction / invalidation.
642:    episode : EpisodicNode
643:        Episode providing content context when extracting edge attributes.
644:    edge_type_candidates : dict[str, type[BaseModel]] | None
645:        Custom edge types permitted for the current source/target signature.
646:
647:    Returns
648:    -------
649:    tuple[EntityEdge, list[EntityEdge], list[EntityEdge]]
650:        The resolved edge, any duplicates, and edges to invalidate.
651:    """
652:    if len(related_edges) == 0 and len(existing_edges) == 0:
653:        # Still extract custom attributes and timestamps even when no dedup needed
654:        edge_model = edge_type_candidates.get(extracted_edge.name) if edge_type_candidates else None
655:        if edge_model is not None and len(edge_model.model_fields) != 0:
656:            edge_attributes_context = {
657:                'fact': extracted_edge.fact,
658:                'reference_time': episode.valid_at if episode is not None else None,
659:                'existing_attributes': extracted_edge.attributes,
660:            }
661:            edge_attributes_response = await llm_client.generate_response(
662:                prompt_library.extract_edges.extract_attributes(edge_attributes_context),
663:                response_model=edge_model,  # type: ignore
664:                model_size=ModelSize.small,
665:                prompt_name='extract_edges.extract_attributes',
666:            )
667:            extracted_edge.attributes = edge_attributes_response
668:
669:        await _extract_edge_timestamps(llm_client, extracted_edge, episode)
670:
671:        return extracted_edge, [], []
672:
673:    # Fast path: if the fact text and endpoints already exist verbatim, reuse the matching edge.
674:    normalized_fact = _normalize_string_exact(extracted_edge.fact)
675:    for edge in related_edges:
676:        if (
677:            edge.source_node_uuid == extracted_edge.source_node_uuid
678:            and edge.target_node_uuid == extracted_edge.target_node_uuid
679:            and _normalize_string_exact(edge.fact) == normalized_fact
680:        ):
681:            resolved = edge
682:            if episode is not None and episode.uuid not in resolved.episodes:
683:                resolved.episodes.append(episode.uuid)
684:            return resolved, [], []
685:
686:    start = time()
687:
688:    # Prepare context for LLM with continuous indexing
689:    related_edges_context = [{'idx': i, 'fact': edge.fact} for i, edge in enumerate(related_edges)]
690:
691:    # Invalidation candidates start where duplicate candidates end
692:    invalidation_idx_offset = len(related_edges)
693:    invalidation_edge_candidates_context = [
694:        {'idx': invalidation_idx_offset + i, 'fact': existing_edge.fact}
695:        for i, existing_edge in enumerate(existing_edges)
696:    ]
697:
698:    context = {
699:        'existing_edges': related_edges_context,
700:        'new_edge': extracted_edge.fact,
701:        'edge_invalidation_candidates': invalidation_edge_candidates_context,
702:    }
703:
704:    if related_edges or existing_edges:
705:        logger.debug(
706:            'Resolving edge: sent %d EXISTING FACTS%s and %d INVALIDATION CANDIDATES%s',
707:            len(related_edges),
708:            f' (idx 0-{len(related_edges) - 1})' if related_edges else '',
709:            len(existing_edges),
710:            f' (idx {invalidation_idx_offset}-{invalidation_idx_offset + len(existing_edges) - 1})'
711:            if existing_edges
712:            else '',
713:        )
714:
715:    llm_response = await llm_client.generate_response(
716:        prompt_library.dedupe_edges.resolve_edge(context),
717:        response_model=EdgeDuplicate,
718:        model_size=ModelSize.small,
719:        prompt_name='dedupe_edges.resolve_edge',
720:    )
721:    response_object = EdgeDuplicate(**llm_response)
722:    duplicate_facts = response_object.duplicate_facts
723:
724:    # Validate duplicate_facts are in valid range for EXISTING FACTS
725:    invalid_duplicates = [i for i in duplicate_facts if i < 0 or i >= len(related_edges)]
726:    if invalid_duplicates:
727:        logger.warning(
728:            'LLM returned invalid duplicate_facts idx values %s (valid range: 0-%d for EXISTING FACTS)',
729:            invalid_duplicates,
730:            len(related_edges) - 1,
731:        )
732:
733:    duplicate_fact_ids: list[int] = [i for i in duplicate_facts if 0 <= i < len(related_edges)]
734:
735:    resolved_edge = extracted_edge
736:    for duplicate_fact_id in duplicate_fact_ids:
737:        resolved_edge = related_edges[duplicate_fact_id]
738:        break
739:
740:    if duplicate_fact_ids and episode is not None:
741:        resolved_edge.episodes.append(episode.uuid)
742:
743:    # Process contradicted facts (continuous indexing across both lists)
744:    contradicted_facts: list[int] = response_object.contradicted_facts
745:    invalidation_candidates: list[EntityEdge] = []
746:
747:    # Only process contradictions if there are edges to check against
748:    if related_edges or existing_edges:
749:        max_valid_idx = len(related_edges) + len(existing_edges) - 1
750:        invalid_contradictions = [i for i in contradicted_facts if i < 0 or i > max_valid_idx]
751:        if invalid_contradictions:
752:            logger.warning(
753:                'LLM returned invalid contradicted_facts idx values %s (valid range: 0-%d)',
754:                invalid_contradictions,
755:                max_valid_idx,
756:            )
757:
758:        # Split contradicted facts into those from related_edges vs existing_edges based on offset
759:        for idx in contradicted_facts:
760:            if 0 <= idx < len(related_edges):
761:                # From EXISTING FACTS (duplicate candidates)
762:                invalidation_candidates.append(related_edges[idx])
763:            elif invalidation_idx_offset <= idx <= max_valid_idx:
764:                # From FACT INVALIDATION CANDIDATES (adjust index by offset)
765:                invalidation_candidates.append(existing_edges[idx - invalidation_idx_offset])
766:
767:    # Only extract structured attributes if the edge's relation_type matches an allowed custom type
768:    # AND the edge model exists for this node pair signature
769:    edge_model = edge_type_candidates.get(resolved_edge.name) if edge_type_candidates else None
770:    if edge_model is not None and len(edge_model.model_fields) != 0:
771:        edge_attributes_context = {
772:            'fact': resolved_edge.fact,
773:            'reference_time': episode.valid_at if episode is not None else None,
774:            'existing_attributes': resolved_edge.attributes,
775:        }
776:
777:        edge_attributes_response = await llm_client.generate_response(
778:            prompt_library.extract_edges.extract_attributes(edge_attributes_context),
779:            response_model=edge_model,  # type: ignore
780:            model_size=ModelSize.small,
781:            prompt_name='extract_edges.extract_attributes',
782:        )
783:
784:        resolved_edge.attributes = edge_attributes_response
785:    else:
786:        resolved_edge.attributes = {}
787:
788:    # Extract timestamps for new edges (duplicated edges retain their existing timestamps)
789:    if resolved_edge.uuid == extracted_edge.uuid:
790:        await _extract_edge_timestamps(llm_client, resolved_edge, episode)
791:
792:    end = time()
793:    logger.debug(
794:        f'Resolved Edge: {extracted_edge.uuid} -> {resolved_edge.uuid}, in {(end - start) * 1000} ms'
795:    )
796:
797:    now = utc_now()
798:
799:    if resolved_edge.invalid_at and not resolved_edge.expired_at:
800:        resolved_edge.expired_at = now
801:
802:    # Determine if the new_edge needs to be expired
803:    if resolved_edge.expired_at is None:
804:        invalidation_candidates.sort(key=lambda c: (c.valid_at is None, ensure_utc(c.valid_at)))
805:        for candidate in invalidation_candidates:
806:            candidate_valid_at_utc = ensure_utc(candidate.valid_at)
807:            resolved_edge_valid_at_utc = ensure_utc(resolved_edge.valid_at)
808:            if (
809:                candidate_valid_at_utc is not None
810:                and resolved_edge_valid_at_utc is not None
811:                and candidate_valid_at_utc > resolved_edge_valid_at_utc
812:            ):
813:                # Expire new edge since we have information about more recent events
814:                resolved_edge.invalid_at = candidate.valid_at
815:                resolved_edge.expired_at = now
816:                break
817:
818:    # Determine which contradictory edges need to be expired
819:    invalidated_edges: list[EntityEdge] = resolve_edge_contradictions(
820:        resolved_edge, invalidation_candidates
821:    )
822:    duplicate_edges: list[EntityEdge] = [related_edges[idx] for idx in duplicate_fact_ids]
823:
824:    return resolved_edge, invalidated_edges, duplicate_edges

827:async def filter_existing_duplicate_of_edges(
828:    driver: GraphDriver, duplicates_node_tuples: list[tuple[EntityNode, EntityNode]]
829:) -> list[tuple[EntityNode, EntityNode]]:
830:    if not duplicates_node_tuples:
831:        return []
832:
833:    duplicate_nodes_map = {
834:        (source.uuid, target.uuid): (source, target) for source, target in duplicates_node_tuples
835:    }
836:
837:    if driver.provider == GraphProvider.NEPTUNE:
838:        query: LiteralString = """
839:            UNWIND $duplicate_node_uuids AS duplicate_tuple
840:            MATCH (n:Entity {uuid: duplicate_tuple.source})-[r:RELATES_TO {name: 'IS_DUPLICATE_OF'}]->(m:Entity {uuid: duplicate_tuple.target})
841:            RETURN DISTINCT
842:                n.uuid AS source_uuid,
843:                m.uuid AS target_uuid
844:        """
845:
846:        duplicate_nodes = [
847:            {'source': source.uuid, 'target': target.uuid}
848:            for source, target in duplicates_node_tuples
849:        ]
850:
851:        records, _, _ = await driver.execute_query(
852:            query,
853:            duplicate_node_uuids=duplicate_nodes,
854:            routing_='r',
855:        )
856:    else:
857:        if driver.provider == GraphProvider.KUZU:
858:            query = """
859:                UNWIND $duplicate_node_uuids AS duplicate
860:                MATCH (n:Entity {uuid: duplicate.src})-[:RELATES_TO]->(e:RelatesToNode_ {name: 'IS_DUPLICATE_OF'})-[:RELATES_TO]->(m:Entity {uuid: duplicate.dst})
861:                RETURN DISTINCT
862:                    n.uuid AS source_uuid,
863:                    m.uuid AS target_uuid
864:            """
865:            duplicate_node_uuids = [{'src': src, 'dst': dst} for src, dst in duplicate_nodes_map]
866:        else:
867:            query: LiteralString = """
868:                UNWIND $duplicate_node_uuids AS duplicate_tuple
869:                MATCH (n:Entity {uuid: duplicate_tuple[0]})-[r:RELATES_TO {name: 'IS_DUPLICATE_OF'}]->(m:Entity {uuid: duplicate_tuple[1]})
870:                RETURN DISTINCT
871:                    n.uuid AS source_uuid,
872:                    m.uuid AS target_uuid
873:            """
874:            duplicate_node_uuids = list(duplicate_nodes_map.keys())
875:
876:        records, _, _ = await driver.execute_query(
877:            query,
878:            duplicate_node_uuids=duplicate_node_uuids,
879:            routing_='r',
880:        )
881:
882:    # Remove duplicates that already have the IS_DUPLICATE_OF edge
883:    for record in records:
884:        duplicate_tuple = (record.get('source_uuid'), record.get('target_uuid'))
885:        if duplicate_nodes_map.get(duplicate_tuple):
886:            duplicate_nodes_map.pop(duplicate_tuple)
887:
888:    return list(duplicate_nodes_map.values())

69:async def extract_nodes(
70:    clients: GraphitiClients,
71:    episode: EpisodicNode | list[EpisodicNode],
72:    previous_episodes: list[EpisodicNode],
73:    entity_types: dict[str, type[BaseModel]] | None = None,
74:    excluded_entity_types: list[str] | None = None,
75:    custom_extraction_instructions: str | None = None,
76:) -> tuple[list[EntityNode], dict[str, list[int]]]:
77:    """Extract entity nodes from one or more episodes.
78:
79:    Parameters
80:    ----------
81:    episode : EpisodicNode | list[EpisodicNode]
82:        A single episode or a list of episodes to extract entities from.
83:        When a list is provided, their contents are concatenated for extraction
84:        and the first episode is used for metadata (source type, group_id, etc.).
85:
86:    Returns
87:    -------
88:    tuple[list[EntityNode], dict[str, list[int]]]
89:        A tuple of (extracted_nodes, node_episode_index_map) where
90:        node_episode_index_map maps node UUID to a list of 0-indexed episode
91:        positions that the node was extracted from.
92:    """
93:    episodes = episode if isinstance(episode, list) else [episode]
94:    primary_episode = episodes[0]
95:
96:    start = time()
97:    llm_client = clients.llm_client
98:
99:    # Build entity types context
100:    entity_types_context = _build_entity_types_context(entity_types)
101:
102:    # Build episode attribution instructions for multi-episode extraction
103:    episode_attribution = ''
104:    if len(episodes) > 1:
105:        episode_attribution = (
106:            '\n7. **Episode Attribution**: The content contains multiple episodes labeled '
107:            '[Episode 0], [Episode 1], etc. Each episode header includes a timestamp indicating '
108:            'when that episode occurred. For each extracted entity, set `episode_indices` '
109:            'to the 0-based list of episode numbers where that entity is mentioned. '
110:            'An entity appearing in Episodes 0 and 2 should have `episode_indices: [0, 2]`.'
111:        )
112:
113:    # Build base context
114:    context = {
115:        'episode_content': concatenate_episodes(episodes),
116:        'episode_timestamp': primary_episode.valid_at.isoformat(),
117:        'previous_episodes': [
118:            {
119:                'content': ep.content,
120:                'timestamp': ep.valid_at.isoformat() if ep.valid_at else None,
121:            }
122:            for ep in previous_episodes
123:        ],
124:        'custom_extraction_instructions': (custom_extraction_instructions or '')
125:        + episode_attribution,
126:        'entity_types': entity_types_context,
127:        'source_description': primary_episode.source_description,
128:    }
129:
130:    # Extract entities
131:    extracted_entities = await _extract_nodes_single(llm_client, primary_episode, context)
132:
133:    # Filter empty names
134:    filtered_entities = [e for e in extracted_entities if e.name.strip()]
135:
136:    end = time()
137:    logger.debug(f'Extracted {len(filtered_entities)} entities in {(end - start) * 1000:.0f} ms')
138:
139:    # Convert to EntityNode objects with episode attribution
140:    extracted_nodes, node_episode_index_map = _create_entity_nodes(
141:        filtered_entities, entity_types_context, excluded_entity_types, episodes
142:    )
143:    extracted_nodes = _collapse_exact_duplicate_extracted_nodes(
144:        extracted_nodes, node_episode_index_map
145:    )
146:
147:    logger.debug(f'Extracted nodes: {[n.uuid for n in extracted_nodes]}')
148:    return extracted_nodes, node_episode_index_map

151:def _build_entity_types_context(
152:    entity_types: dict[str, type[BaseModel]] | None,
153:) -> list[dict]:
154:    """Build entity types context with ID mappings."""
155:    entity_types_context = [
156:        {
157:            'entity_type_id': 0,
158:            'entity_type_name': 'Entity',
159:            'entity_type_description': (
160:                'A specific, identifiable entity that does not fit any of the other listed '
161:                'types. Must still be a concrete, meaningful thing — specific enough to be '
162:                'uniquely identifiable. GOOD: a named entity not covered by the other types. '
163:                'BAD: "luck", "ideas", "tomorrow", "things", "them", "everybody", '
164:                '"a sense of wonder", "great times". '
165:                'When in doubt, do not extract the entity.'
166:            ),
167:        }
168:    ]
169:
170:    if entity_types is not None:
171:        entity_types_context += [
172:            {
173:                'entity_type_id': i + 1,
174:                'entity_type_name': type_name,
175:                'entity_type_description': type_model.__doc__,
176:            }
177:            for i, (type_name, type_model) in enumerate(entity_types.items())
178:        ]
179:
180:    return entity_types_context

183:def _get_entity_type_description(
184:    labels: list[str], entity_types: dict[str, type[BaseModel]] | None
185:) -> str:
186:    type_name = next((item for item in labels if item != 'Entity'), '')
187:    type_model = entity_types.get(type_name) if entity_types is not None else None
188:    return (type_model.__doc__ if type_model is not None else None) or 'Default Entity Type'

190:
191:def _truncate_type_description(docstring: str) -> str:
192:    """Extract a concise type description from a docstring for summary prompts.
193:
194:    Returns the first paragraph (up to the first blank line), capped at 3
195:    sentences.  This strips GOOD/BAD examples, trigger patterns, and other
196:    extraction-specific guidance that is irrelevant to summarization.
197:    """
198:    # Take only the first paragraph.
199:    paragraph_lines: list[str] = []
200:    for line in docstring.splitlines():
201:        if not line.strip():
202:            if paragraph_lines:
203:                break
204:            continue  # skip leading blank lines
205:        paragraph_lines.append(line)
206:
207:    text = ' '.join(line.strip() for line in paragraph_lines)
208:
209:    # Cap at 3 sentences.
210:    sentences: list[str] = []
211:    remaining = text
212:    for _ in range(3):
213:        idx = _find_sentence_end(remaining)
214:        if idx == -1:
215:            sentences.append(remaining)
216:            remaining = ''
217:            break
218:        sentences.append(remaining[: idx + 1])
219:        remaining = remaining[idx + 1 :].lstrip()
220:    return ' '.join(sentences).strip()
221:
222:
223:def _find_sentence_end(text: str) -> int:
224:    """Return the index of the first sentence boundary.
225:
226:    A sentence ends at `.`, `!`, or `?` when followed by end-of-string or
227:    a space then an uppercase letter.  This avoids splitting on abbreviations
228:    like "e.g.", "Dr.", or decimals like "2.0".
229:    """
230:    n = len(text)
231:    for i, ch in enumerate(text):
232:        if ch not in '.!?':
233:            continue
234:        # End of string counts as a sentence boundary.
235:        if i + 1 >= n:
236:            return i
237:        # Space followed by an uppercase letter is a sentence boundary.
238:        if text[i + 1] == ' ' and i + 2 < n and text[i + 2].isupper():
239:            return i
240:    return -1

243:async def _extract_nodes_single(
244:    llm_client: LLMClient,
245:    episode: EpisodicNode,
246:    context: dict,
247:) -> list[ExtractedEntity]:
248:    """Extract entities using a single LLM call."""
249:    llm_response = await _call_extraction_llm(llm_client, episode, context)
250:    response_object = ExtractedEntities(**llm_response)
251:    return response_object.extracted_entities

253:
254:async def _call_extraction_llm(
255:    llm_client: LLMClient,
256:    episode: EpisodicNode,
257:    context: dict,
258:) -> dict:
259:    """Call the appropriate extraction prompt based on episode type."""
260:    if episode.source == EpisodeType.message:
261:        prompt = prompt_library.extract_nodes.extract_message(context)
262:        prompt_name = 'extract_nodes.extract_message'
263:    elif episode.source == EpisodeType.text:
264:        prompt = prompt_library.extract_nodes.extract_text(context)
265:        prompt_name = 'extract_nodes.extract_text'
266:    elif episode.source == EpisodeType.json:
267:        prompt = prompt_library.extract_nodes.extract_json(context)
268:        prompt_name = 'extract_nodes.extract_json'
269:    else:
270:        # Fallback to text extraction
271:        prompt = prompt_library.extract_nodes.extract_text(context)
272:        prompt_name = 'extract_nodes.extract_text'
273:
274:    return await llm_client.generate_response(
275:        prompt,
276:        response_model=ExtractedEntities,
277:        group_id=episode.group_id,
278:        prompt_name=prompt_name,
279:    )

282:def _create_entity_nodes(
283:    extracted_entities: list[ExtractedEntity],
284:    entity_types_context: list[dict],
285:    excluded_entity_types: list[str] | None,
286:    episodes: list[EpisodicNode],
287:) -> tuple[list[EntityNode], dict[str, list[int]]]:
288:    """Convert ExtractedEntity objects to EntityNode objects.
289:
290:    Returns
291:    -------
292:    tuple[list[EntityNode], dict[str, list[int]]]
293:        A tuple of (nodes, node_episode_index_map) where node_episode_index_map
294:        maps each node UUID to 0-indexed episode positions the node was extracted from.
295:    """
296:    primary_episode = episodes[0]
297:    extracted_nodes = []
298:    node_episode_index_map: dict[str, list[int]] = {}
299:
300:    for extracted_entity in extracted_entities:
301:        type_id = extracted_entity.entity_type_id
302:        if 0 <= type_id < len(entity_types_context):
303:            entity_type_name = entity_types_context[type_id].get('entity_type_name')
304:        else:
305:            entity_type_name = 'Entity'
306:
307:        # Check if this entity type should be excluded
308:        if excluded_entity_types and entity_type_name in excluded_entity_types:
309:            logger.debug(f'Excluding entity of type "{entity_type_name}"')
310:            continue
311:
312:        labels: list[str] = list({'Entity', str(entity_type_name)})
313:
314:        new_node = EntityNode(
315:            name=extracted_entity.name,
316:            group_id=primary_episode.group_id,
317:            labels=labels,
318:            summary='',
319:            created_at=utc_now(),
320:        )
321:        extracted_nodes.append(new_node)
322:
323:        # Map node to 0-indexed episode positions (LLM returns 0-indexed).
324:        # Clamp to valid range; fall back to all episodes if empty.
325:        indices = [i for i in extracted_entity.episode_indices if 0 <= i < len(episodes)]
326:        if not indices:
327:            indices = list(range(len(episodes)))
328:        node_episode_index_map[new_node.uuid] = indices
329:
330:        logger.debug(f'Created new node: {new_node.uuid}')
331:
332:    return extracted_nodes, node_episode_index_map
333:
334:
335:def _collapse_exact_duplicate_extracted_nodes(
336:    extracted_nodes: list[EntityNode],
337:    node_episode_index_map: dict[str, list[int]] | None = None,
338:) -> list[EntityNode]:
339:    """Collapse same-message duplicates with the same normalized name.
340:
341:    This is intentionally narrow: it only merges exact normalized-name duplicates that the
342:    extraction prompt should already have emitted once. When duplicates disagree on specificity,
343:    keep the more specific node (for example, `Person` over bare `Entity`).
344:
345:    When node_episode_index_map is provided, episode indices from discarded nodes are merged
346:    into the canonical node's entry so attribution is preserved.
347:    """
348:    if len(extracted_nodes) < 2:
349:        return extracted_nodes
350:
351:    canonical_by_name: dict[str, EntityNode] = {}
352:    ordered_names: list[str] = []
353:
354:    for node in extracted_nodes:
355:        normalized_name = _normalize_string_exact(node.name)
356:        existing = canonical_by_name.get(normalized_name)
357:        if existing is None:
358:            canonical_by_name[normalized_name] = node
359:            ordered_names.append(normalized_name)
360:            continue
361:
362:        existing_specific_labels = {label for label in existing.labels if label != 'Entity'}
363:        node_specific_labels = {label for label in node.labels if label != 'Entity'}
364:        if len(node_specific_labels) > len(existing_specific_labels) or (
365:            len(node_specific_labels) == len(existing_specific_labels)
366:            and len(node.name.strip()) > len(existing.name.strip())
367:        ):
368:            old_canonical = existing
369:            canonical_by_name[normalized_name] = node
370:            # Merge episode indices: old canonical -> new canonical
371:            if node_episode_index_map is not None:
372:                old_indices = node_episode_index_map.pop(old_canonical.uuid, [])
373:                new_indices = node_episode_index_map.get(node.uuid, [])
374:                node_episode_index_map[node.uuid] = sorted(set(new_indices + old_indices))
375:        elif node_episode_index_map is not None:
376:            # Discard this node; merge its indices into the existing canonical
377:            discarded_indices = node_episode_index_map.pop(node.uuid, [])
378:            canonical_indices = node_episode_index_map.get(existing.uuid, [])
379:            node_episode_index_map[existing.uuid] = sorted(
380:                set(canonical_indices + discarded_indices)
381:            )
382:
383:    return [canonical_by_name[name] for name in ordered_names]
384:
385:
386:def _merge_candidate_nodes(
387:    candidate_nodes: list[EntityNode],
388:    existing_nodes_override: list[EntityNode] | None,
389:) -> list[EntityNode]:
390:    """Deduplicate candidate nodes while preserving search order and overrides."""
391:    merged_candidates = list(candidate_nodes)
392:    if existing_nodes_override is not None:
393:        merged_candidates.extend(existing_nodes_override)
394:
395:    seen_candidate_uuids: set[str] = set()
396:    ordered_candidates: list[EntityNode] = []
397:    for candidate in merged_candidates:
398:        if candidate.uuid in seen_candidate_uuids:
399:            continue
400:        seen_candidate_uuids.add(candidate.uuid)
401:        ordered_candidates.append(candidate)
402:
403:    return ordered_candidates
404:
405:
406:async def _collect_candidate_nodes(
407:    clients: GraphitiClients,
408:    extracted_nodes: list[EntityNode],
409:    existing_nodes_override: list[EntityNode] | None,
410:) -> list[list[EntityNode]]:
411:    """Search per extracted name and return ordered candidates for each extracted node."""
412:    search_results = await _semantic_candidate_search(clients, extracted_nodes)
413:
414:    return [_merge_candidate_nodes(result, existing_nodes_override) for result in search_results]
415:
416:
417:async def _semantic_candidate_search(
418:    clients: GraphitiClients,
419:    extracted_nodes: list[EntityNode],
420:) -> list[list[EntityNode]]:
421:    """Run direct cosine similarity search per extracted node without reranking."""
422:    if not extracted_nodes:
423:        return []
424:
425:    queries = [node.name.replace('\n', ' ') for node in extracted_nodes]
426:    try:
427:        query_vectors = await clients.embedder.create_batch(queries)
428:    except NotImplementedError:
429:        query_vectors = list(
430:            await semaphore_gather(
431:                *[clients.embedder.create(input_data=[query]) for query in queries]
432:            )
433:        )
434:
435:    return list(
436:        await semaphore_gather(
437:            *[
438:                node_similarity_search(
439:                    clients.driver,
440:                    query_vector,
441:                    SearchFilters(),
442:                    [node.group_id],
443:                    NODE_DEDUP_CANDIDATE_LIMIT,
444:                    NODE_DEDUP_COSINE_MIN_SCORE,
445:                )
446:                for node, query_vector in zip(extracted_nodes, query_vectors, strict=True)
447:            ]
448:        )
449:    )
450:
451:
452:def _commit_resolution(
453:    state: DedupResolutionState,
454:    resolved_node: EntityNode | None,
455:    uuid_map: dict[str, str],
456:    duplicate_pairs: list[tuple[EntityNode, EntityNode]],
457:    index: int,
458:) -> None:
459:    """Commit a single-node resolution result into the batch-level state."""
460:    if resolved_node is not None:
461:        state.resolved_nodes[index] = resolved_node
462:    state.uuid_map.update(uuid_map)
463:    state.duplicate_pairs.extend(duplicate_pairs)

465:
466:async def _resolve_with_llm(
467:    llm_client: LLMClient,
468:    extracted_nodes: list[EntityNode],
469:    indexes: DedupCandidateIndexes,
470:    state: DedupResolutionState,
471:    episode: EpisodicNode | None,
472:    previous_episodes: list[EpisodicNode] | None,
473:    entity_types: dict[str, type[BaseModel]] | None,
474:) -> None:
475:    """Escalate unresolved nodes to the dedupe prompt so the LLM can select or reject duplicates.
476:
477:    The guardrails below defensively ignore malformed or duplicate LLM responses so the
478:    ingestion workflow remains deterministic even when the model misbehaves.
479:    """
480:    if not state.unresolved_indices:
481:        return
482:
483:    entity_types_dict: dict[str, type[BaseModel]] = entity_types if entity_types is not None else {}
484:
485:    llm_extracted_nodes = [extracted_nodes[i] for i in state.unresolved_indices]
486:
487:    extracted_nodes_context = [
488:        {
489:            'id': i,
490:            'name': node.name,
491:            'entity_type': node.labels,
492:            'entity_type_description': _get_entity_type_description(node.labels, entity_types_dict),
493:        }
494:        for i, node in enumerate(llm_extracted_nodes)
495:    ]
496:
497:    sent_ids = [ctx['id'] for ctx in extracted_nodes_context]
498:    logger.debug(
499:        'Sending %d entities to LLM for deduplication with IDs 0-%d (actual IDs sent: %s)',
500:        len(llm_extracted_nodes),
501:        len(llm_extracted_nodes) - 1,
502:        sent_ids if len(sent_ids) < 20 else f'{sent_ids[:10]}...{sent_ids[-10:]}',
503:    )
504:    if llm_extracted_nodes:
505:        sample_size = min(3, len(extracted_nodes_context))
506:        logger.debug(
507:            'First %d entity IDs: %s',
508:            sample_size,
509:            [ctx['id'] for ctx in extracted_nodes_context[:sample_size]],
510:        )
511:        if len(extracted_nodes_context) > 3:
512:            logger.debug(
513:                'Last %d entity IDs: %s',
514:                sample_size,
515:                [ctx['id'] for ctx in extracted_nodes_context[-sample_size:]],
516:            )
517:
518:    existing_nodes_context = [
519:        {
520:            **candidate.attributes,
521:            'candidate_id': i,
522:            'name': candidate.name,
523:            'entity_types': candidate.labels,
524:            'summary': candidate.summary[:120] if candidate.summary else '',
525:        }
526:        for i, candidate in enumerate(indexes.existing_nodes)
527:    ]
528:
529:    # Build candidate_id -> node mapping for resolving duplicates by ID
530:    candidates_by_id: dict[int, EntityNode] = {
531:        i: node for i, node in enumerate(indexes.existing_nodes)
532:    }
533:
534:    context = {
535:        'extracted_nodes': extracted_nodes_context,
536:        'existing_nodes': existing_nodes_context,
537:        'episode_content': episode.content if episode is not None else '',
538:        'previous_episodes': (
539:            [
540:                {
541:                    'content': ep.content,
542:                    'timestamp': ep.valid_at.isoformat() if ep.valid_at else None,
543:                }
544:                for ep in previous_episodes
545:            ]
546:            if previous_episodes is not None
547:            else []
548:        ),
549:    }
550:
551:    llm_response = await llm_client.generate_response(
552:        prompt_library.dedupe_nodes.nodes(context),
553:        response_model=NodeResolutions,
554:        prompt_name='dedupe_nodes.nodes',
555:    )
556:
557:    node_resolutions: list[NodeDuplicate] = NodeResolutions(**llm_response).entity_resolutions
558:
559:    valid_relative_range = range(len(state.unresolved_indices))
560:    processed_relative_ids: set[int] = set()
561:
562:    received_ids = {r.id for r in node_resolutions}
563:    expected_ids = set(valid_relative_range)
564:    missing_ids = expected_ids - received_ids
565:    extra_ids = received_ids - expected_ids
566:
567:    logger.debug(
568:        'Received %d resolutions for %d entities',
569:        len(node_resolutions),
570:        len(state.unresolved_indices),
571:    )
572:
573:    if missing_ids:
574:        logger.warning('LLM did not return resolutions for IDs: %s', sorted(missing_ids))
575:
576:    if extra_ids:
577:        logger.warning(
578:            'LLM returned invalid IDs outside valid range 0-%d: %s (all returned IDs: %s)',
579:            len(state.unresolved_indices) - 1,
580:            sorted(extra_ids),
581:            sorted(received_ids),
582:        )
583:
584:    for resolution in node_resolutions:
585:        relative_id: int = resolution.id
586:        duplicate_candidate_id: int = resolution.duplicate_candidate_id
587:
588:        if relative_id not in valid_relative_range:
589:            logger.warning(
590:                'Skipping invalid LLM dedupe id %d (valid range: 0-%d, received %d resolutions)',
591:                relative_id,
592:                len(state.unresolved_indices) - 1,
593:                len(node_resolutions),
594:            )
595:            continue
596:
597:        if relative_id in processed_relative_ids:
598:            logger.warning('Duplicate LLM dedupe id %s received; ignoring.', relative_id)
599:            continue
600:        processed_relative_ids.add(relative_id)
601:
602:        original_index = state.unresolved_indices[relative_id]
603:        extracted_node = extracted_nodes[original_index]
604:
605:        resolved_node: EntityNode
606:        if duplicate_candidate_id < 0:
607:            resolved_node = extracted_node
608:        elif duplicate_candidate_id in candidates_by_id:
609:            resolved_node = _promote_resolved_node(
610:                extracted_node, candidates_by_id[duplicate_candidate_id]
611:            )
612:        else:
613:            logger.warning(
614:                'Invalid duplicate_candidate_id %d for extracted node %s; treating as no duplicate.',
615:                duplicate_candidate_id,
616:                extracted_node.uuid,
617:            )
618:            resolved_node = extracted_node
619:
620:        state.resolved_nodes[original_index] = resolved_node
621:        state.uuid_map[extracted_node.uuid] = resolved_node.uuid
622:        if resolved_node.uuid != extracted_node.uuid:
623:            state.duplicate_pairs.append((extracted_node, resolved_node))
624:
625:
626:async def resolve_extracted_nodes(
627:    clients: GraphitiClients,
628:    extracted_nodes: list[EntityNode],
629:    episode: EpisodicNode | None = None,
630:    previous_episodes: list[EpisodicNode] | None = None,
631:    entity_types: dict[str, type[BaseModel]] | None = None,
632:    existing_nodes_override: list[EntityNode] | None = None,
633:) -> tuple[list[EntityNode], dict[str, str], list[tuple[EntityNode, EntityNode]]]:
634:    """Resolve nodes with semantic retrieval first, then deterministic and LLM dedup."""
635:    llm_client = clients.llm_client
636:    candidate_nodes_by_extracted = await _collect_candidate_nodes(
637:        clients,
638:        extracted_nodes,
639:        existing_nodes_override,
640:    )
641:
642:    state = DedupResolutionState(
643:        resolved_nodes=[None] * len(extracted_nodes),
644:        uuid_map={},
645:        unresolved_indices=[],
646:    )
647:
648:    for idx, (node, candidates) in enumerate(
649:        zip(extracted_nodes, candidate_nodes_by_extracted, strict=True)
650:    ):
651:        if not candidates:
652:            continue
653:
654:        indexes = _build_candidate_indexes(candidates)
655:        local_state = DedupResolutionState(
656:            resolved_nodes=[None], uuid_map={}, unresolved_indices=[]
657:        )
658:        _resolve_with_similarity([node], indexes, local_state)
659:        if local_state.resolved_nodes[0] is not None:
660:            _commit_resolution(
661:                state,
662:                local_state.resolved_nodes[0],
663:                local_state.uuid_map,
664:                local_state.duplicate_pairs,
665:                idx,
666:            )
667:            continue
668:
669:        state.unresolved_indices.append(idx)
670:
671:    if state.unresolved_indices:
672:        llm_candidate_nodes = _merge_candidate_nodes(
673:            [
674:                candidate
675:                for idx in state.unresolved_indices
676:                for candidate in candidate_nodes_by_extracted[idx]
677:            ],
678:            None,
679:        )
680:        await _resolve_with_llm(
681:            llm_client,
682:            extracted_nodes,
683:            _build_candidate_indexes(llm_candidate_nodes),
684:            state,
685:            episode,
686:            previous_episodes,
687:            entity_types,
688:        )
689:
690:    if not state.unresolved_indices and not any(candidate_nodes_by_extracted):
691:        logger.debug('No semantic dedup candidates found; keeping all extracted nodes as new')
692:
693:    for idx, node in enumerate(extracted_nodes):
694:        if state.resolved_nodes[idx] is None:
695:            state.resolved_nodes[idx] = node
696:            state.uuid_map[node.uuid] = node.uuid
697:
698:    logger.debug(
699:        'Resolved nodes: %s',
700:        [node.uuid for node in state.resolved_nodes if node is not None],
701:    )
702:
703:    return (
704:        [node for node in state.resolved_nodes if node is not None],
705:        state.uuid_map,
706:        state.duplicate_pairs,
707:    )
708:
709:
710:def _build_edges_by_node(edges: list[EntityEdge] | None) -> dict[str, list[EntityEdge]]:
711:    """Build a dictionary mapping node UUIDs to their connected edges."""
712:    edges_by_node: dict[str, list[EntityEdge]] = {}
713:    if not edges:
714:        return edges_by_node
715:    for edge in edges:
716:        if edge.source_node_uuid not in edges_by_node:
717:            edges_by_node[edge.source_node_uuid] = []
718:        if edge.target_node_uuid not in edges_by_node:
719:            edges_by_node[edge.target_node_uuid] = []
720:        edges_by_node[edge.source_node_uuid].append(edge)
721:        edges_by_node[edge.target_node_uuid].append(edge)
722:    return edges_by_node

724:
725:async def extract_attributes_from_nodes(
726:    clients: GraphitiClients,
727:    nodes: list[EntityNode],
728:    episode: EpisodicNode | list[EpisodicNode] | None = None,
729:    previous_episodes: list[EpisodicNode] | None = None,
730:    entity_types: dict[str, type[BaseModel]] | None = None,
731:    should_summarize_node: NodeSummaryFilter | None = None,
732:    edges: list[EntityEdge] | None = None,
733:    skip_fact_appending: bool = False,
734:    include_type_descriptions: bool = False,
735:) -> list[EntityNode]:
736:    llm_client = clients.llm_client
737:    embedder = clients.embedder
738:
739:    # Pre-build edges lookup for O(E + N) instead of O(N * E)
740:    edges_by_node = _build_edges_by_node(edges)
741:
742:    # Extract attributes in parallel (per-entity calls)
743:    attribute_results: list[dict[str, Any]] = await semaphore_gather(
744:        *[
745:            _extract_entity_attributes(
746:                llm_client,
747:                node,
748:                episode,
749:                previous_episodes,
750:                (
751:                    entity_types.get(next((item for item in node.labels if item != 'Entity'), ''))
752:                    if entity_types is not None
753:                    else None
754:                ),
755:            )
756:            for node in nodes
757:        ]
758:    )
759:
760:    # Apply attributes to nodes
761:    for node, attributes in zip(nodes, attribute_results, strict=True):
762:        node.attributes.update(attributes)
763:
764:    # Extract summaries in batch
765:    await _extract_entity_summaries_batch(
766:        llm_client,
767:        nodes,
768:        episode,
769:        previous_episodes,
770:        should_summarize_node,
771:        edges_by_node,
772:        skip_fact_appending=skip_fact_appending,
773:        entity_types=entity_types if include_type_descriptions else None,
774:    )
775:
776:    await create_entity_node_embeddings(embedder, nodes)
777:
778:    return nodes

781:async def _extract_entity_attributes(
782:    llm_client: LLMClient,
783:    node: EntityNode,
784:    episode: EpisodicNode | list[EpisodicNode] | None,
785:    previous_episodes: list[EpisodicNode] | None,
786:    entity_type: type[BaseModel] | None,
787:) -> dict[str, Any]:
788:    if entity_type is None or len(entity_type.model_fields) == 0:
789:        return {}
790:
791:    attributes_context = _build_episode_context(
792:        # should not include summary
793:        node_data={
794:            'name': node.name,
795:            'entity_types': node.labels,
796:            'attributes': node.attributes,
797:        },
798:        episode=episode,
799:        previous_episodes=previous_episodes,
800:    )
801:
802:    llm_response = await llm_client.generate_response(
803:        prompt_library.extract_nodes.extract_attributes(attributes_context),
804:        response_model=entity_type,
805:        model_size=ModelSize.small,
806:        group_id=node.group_id,
807:        prompt_name='extract_nodes.extract_attributes',
808:    )
809:
810:    # validate response
811:    entity_type(**llm_response)
812:
813:    return llm_response
