815:
816:async def _extract_entity_summaries_batch(
817:    llm_client: LLMClient,
818:    nodes: list[EntityNode],
819:    episode: EpisodicNode | list[EpisodicNode] | None,
820:    previous_episodes: list[EpisodicNode] | None,
821:    should_summarize_node: NodeSummaryFilter | None,
822:    edges_by_node: dict[str, list[EntityEdge]],
823:    *,
824:    skip_fact_appending: bool = False,
825:    entity_types: dict[str, type[BaseModel]] | None = None,
826:) -> None:
827:    """Extract summaries for multiple entities in batched LLM calls.
828:
829:    When skip_fact_appending is False (default), nodes with short summaries get edge
830:    facts appended directly without an LLM call.  Nodes needing summarization are
831:    partitioned into flights of MAX_NODES and processed with separate LLM calls.
832:
833:    When skip_fact_appending is True, the raw fact-append shortcut is bypassed and all
834:    nodes are routed through LLM summarization using an episode-based prompt that
835:    matches the async graph summary worker.
836:    """
837:    # Determine which nodes need LLM summarization vs direct edge fact appending
838:    nodes_needing_llm: list[EntityNode] = []
839:
840:    for node in nodes:
841:        # Check if node should be summarized at all
842:        if should_summarize_node is not None and not await should_summarize_node(node):
843:            continue
844:
845:        if skip_fact_appending:
846:            # Always route through LLM — no raw fact concatenation.
847:            if episode is not None or node.summary:
848:                nodes_needing_llm.append(node)
849:            continue
850:
851:        node_edges = edges_by_node.get(node.uuid, [])
852:
853:        # Build summary with edge facts appended
854:        summary_with_edges = node.summary
855:        if node_edges:
856:            edge_facts = '\n'.join(edge.fact for edge in node_edges if edge.fact)
857:            summary_with_edges = f'{summary_with_edges}\n{edge_facts}'.strip()
858:
859:        # If summary is close to the persisted limit, use it directly (append edge facts, no LLM call)
860:        if summary_with_edges and len(summary_with_edges) <= MAX_SUMMARY_CHARS * 2:
861:            node.summary = summary_with_edges
862:            continue
863:
864:        # Skip if no summary content and no episode to generate from
865:        if not summary_with_edges and episode is None:
866:            continue
867:
868:        # This node needs LLM summarization
869:        nodes_needing_llm.append(node)
870:
871:    # If no nodes need LLM summarization, return early
872:    if not nodes_needing_llm:
873:        return
874:
875:    # Partition nodes into flights of MAX_NODES
876:    node_flights = [
877:        nodes_needing_llm[i : i + MAX_NODES] for i in range(0, len(nodes_needing_llm), MAX_NODES)
878:    ]
879:
880:    # Process flights in parallel
881:    await semaphore_gather(
882:        *[
883:            _process_summary_flight(
884:                llm_client,
885:                flight,
886:                episode,
887:                previous_episodes,
888:                use_episode_prompt=skip_fact_appending,
889:                entity_types=entity_types,
890:            )
891:            for flight in node_flights
892:        ]
893:    )
894:
895:
896:async def _process_summary_flight(
897:    llm_client: LLMClient,
898:    nodes: list[EntityNode],
899:    episode: EpisodicNode | list[EpisodicNode] | None,
900:    previous_episodes: list[EpisodicNode] | None,
901:    *,
902:    use_episode_prompt: bool = False,
903:    entity_types: dict[str, type[BaseModel]] | None = None,
904:) -> None:
905:    """Process a single flight of nodes for batch summarization."""
906:    # Build entity type descriptions from docstrings, stripping GOOD/BAD
907:    # few-shot examples that are intended for extraction prompts only.
908:    entity_type_descriptions: dict[str, str] = {}
909:    if entity_types is not None:
910:        for type_name, type_model in entity_types.items():
911:            if type_model.__doc__:
912:                entity_type_descriptions[type_name] = _truncate_type_description(type_model.__doc__)
913:
914:    # Build context for batch summarization
915:    entities_context = [
916:        {
917:            'name': node.name,
918:            'summary': node.summary,
919:            'entity_types': node.labels,
920:            'attributes': node.attributes,
921:        }
922:        for node in nodes
923:    ]
924:
925:    if episode is None:
926:        episode_content = ''
927:    elif isinstance(episode, list):
928:        episode_content = concatenate_episodes(episode)
929:    else:
930:        episode_content = episode.content
931:
932:    batch_context: dict[str, Any] = {
933:        'entities': entities_context,
934:        'episode_content': episode_content,
935:        'previous_episodes': (
936:            [
937:                {
938:                    'content': ep.content,
939:                    'timestamp': ep.valid_at.isoformat() if ep.valid_at else None,
940:                }
941:                for ep in previous_episodes
942:            ]
943:            if previous_episodes is not None
944:            else []
945:        ),
946:        'entity_type_descriptions': entity_type_descriptions,
947:    }
948:
949:    # Get group_id from the first node (all nodes in a batch should have same group_id)
950:    group_id = nodes[0].group_id if nodes else None
951:
952:    if use_episode_prompt:
953:        prompt = prompt_library.extract_nodes.extract_entity_summaries_from_episodes(batch_context)
954:        prompt_name = 'extract_nodes.extract_entity_summaries_from_episodes'
955:    else:
956:        prompt = prompt_library.extract_nodes.extract_summaries_batch(batch_context)
957:        prompt_name = 'extract_nodes.extract_summaries_batch'
958:
959:    llm_response = await llm_client.generate_response(
960:        prompt,
961:        response_model=SummarizedEntities,
962:        model_size=ModelSize.small,
963:        group_id=group_id,
964:        prompt_name=prompt_name,
965:    )
966:
967:    # Build case-insensitive name -> nodes mapping (handles duplicates)
968:    name_to_nodes: dict[str, list[EntityNode]] = {}
969:    for node in nodes:
970:        key = node.name.lower()
971:        if key not in name_to_nodes:
972:            name_to_nodes[key] = []
973:        name_to_nodes[key].append(node)
974:
975:    # Apply summaries from LLM response
976:    summaries_response = SummarizedEntities(**llm_response)
977:    for summarized_entity in summaries_response.summaries:
978:        matching_nodes = name_to_nodes.get(summarized_entity.name.lower(), [])
979:        if matching_nodes:
980:            truncated_summary = truncate_at_sentence(summarized_entity.summary, MAX_SUMMARY_CHARS)
981:            for node in matching_nodes:
982:                node.summary = truncated_summary
983:        else:
984:            logger.warning(
985:                'LLM returned summary for unknown entity (first 30 chars): %.30s',
986:                summarized_entity.name,
987:            )
988:
989:
990:def _build_episode_context(
991:    node_data: dict[str, Any],
992:    episode: EpisodicNode | list[EpisodicNode] | None,
993:    previous_episodes: list[EpisodicNode] | None,
994:) -> dict[str, Any]:
995:    if episode is None:
996:        episode_content = ''
997:    elif isinstance(episode, list):
998:        episode_content = concatenate_episodes(episode)
999:    else:
1000:        episode_content = episode.content
1001:    return {
1002:        'node': node_data,
1003:        'episode_content': episode_content,
1004:        'previous_episodes': (
1005:            [
1006:                {
1007:                    'content': ep.content,
1008:                    'timestamp': ep.valid_at.isoformat() if ep.valid_at else None,
1009:                }
1010:                for ep in previous_episodes
1011:            ]
1012:            if previous_episodes is not None
1013:            else []
1014:        ),
1015:    }

36:class FalkorGraphMaintenanceOperations(GraphMaintenanceOperations):
37:    async def clear_data(
38:        self,
39:        executor: QueryExecutor,
40:        group_ids: list[str] | None = None,
41:    ) -> None:
42:        if group_ids is None:
43:            await executor.execute_query('MATCH (n) DETACH DELETE n')
44:        else:
45:            # FalkorDB: iterate labels individually
46:            for label in ['Entity', 'Episodic', 'Community']:
47:                await executor.execute_query(
48:                    f"""
49:                    MATCH (n:{label})
50:                    WHERE n.group_id IN $group_ids
51:                    DETACH DELETE n
52:                    """,
53:                    group_ids=group_ids,
54:                )
55:
56:    async def build_indices_and_constraints(
57:        self,
58:        executor: QueryExecutor,
59:        delete_existing: bool = False,
60:    ) -> None:
61:        if delete_existing:
62:            await self.delete_all_indexes(executor)
63:
64:        range_indices = get_range_indices(GraphProvider.FALKORDB)
65:        fulltext_indices = get_fulltext_indices(GraphProvider.FALKORDB)
66:        index_queries = range_indices + fulltext_indices
67:
68:        # FalkorDB executes indices sequentially (catches "already indexed" in execute_query)
69:        for query in index_queries:
70:            await executor.execute_query(query)
71:
72:    async def delete_all_indexes(
73:        self,
74:        executor: QueryExecutor,
75:    ) -> None:
76:        result = await executor.execute_query('CALL db.indexes()')
77:        if not result:
78:            return
79:
80:        records, _, _ = result
81:        drop_tasks = []
82:
83:        for record in records:
84:            label = record['label']
85:            entity_type = record['entitytype']
86:
87:            for field_name, index_type in record['types'].items():
88:                if 'RANGE' in index_type:
89:                    drop_tasks.append(
90:                        executor.execute_query(f'DROP INDEX ON :{label}({field_name})')
91:                    )
92:                elif 'FULLTEXT' in index_type:
93:                    if entity_type == 'NODE':
94:                        drop_tasks.append(
95:                            executor.execute_query(
96:                                f'DROP FULLTEXT INDEX FOR (n:{label}) ON (n.{field_name})'
97:                            )
98:                        )
99:                    elif entity_type == 'RELATIONSHIP':
100:                        drop_tasks.append(
101:                            executor.execute_query(
102:                                f'DROP FULLTEXT INDEX FOR ()-[e:{label}]-() ON (e.{field_name})'
103:                            )
104:                        )
105:
106:        if drop_tasks:
107:            await asyncio.gather(*drop_tasks)
108:
109:    async def get_community_clusters(
110:        self,
111:        executor: QueryExecutor,
112:        group_ids: list[str] | None = None,
113:    ) -> list[Any]:
114:        community_clusters: list[list[EntityNode]] = []
115:
116:        if group_ids is None:
117:            group_id_values, _, _ = await executor.execute_query(
118:                """
119:                MATCH (n:Entity)
120:                WHERE n.group_id IS NOT NULL
121:                RETURN
122:                    collect(DISTINCT n.group_id) AS group_ids
123:                """
124:            )
125:            group_ids = group_id_values[0]['group_ids'] if group_id_values else []
126:
127:        resolved_group_ids: list[str] = group_ids or []
128:        for group_id in resolved_group_ids:
129:            projection: dict[str, list[Neighbor]] = {}
130:
131:            node_records, _, _ = await executor.execute_query(
132:                """
133:                MATCH (n:Entity)
134:                WHERE n.group_id IN $group_ids
135:                RETURN
136:                """
137:                + get_entity_node_return_query(GraphProvider.FALKORDB),
138:                group_ids=[group_id],
139:            )
140:            nodes = [entity_node_from_record(r) for r in node_records]
141:
142:            for node in nodes:
143:                records, _, _ = await executor.execute_query(
144:                    """
145:                    MATCH (n:Entity {group_id: $group_id, uuid: $uuid})-[e:RELATES_TO]-(m: Entity {group_id: $group_id})
146:                    WITH count(e) AS count, m.uuid AS uuid
147:                    RETURN
148:                        uuid,
149:                        count
150:                    """,
151:                    uuid=node.uuid,
152:                    group_id=group_id,
153:                )
154:
155:                projection[node.uuid] = [
156:                    Neighbor(node_uuid=record['uuid'], edge_count=record['count'])
157:                    for record in records
158:                ]
159:
160:            cluster_uuids = label_propagation(projection)
161:
162:            for cluster in cluster_uuids:
163:                if not cluster:
164:                    continue
165:                cluster_records, _, _ = await executor.execute_query(
166:                    """
167:                    MATCH (n:Entity)
168:                    WHERE n.uuid IN $uuids
169:                    RETURN
170:                    """
171:                    + get_entity_node_return_query(GraphProvider.FALKORDB),
172:                    uuids=cluster,
173:                )
174:                community_clusters.append([entity_node_from_record(r) for r in cluster_records])
175:
176:        return community_clusters

178:    async def remove_communities(
179:        self,
180:        executor: QueryExecutor,
181:    ) -> None:
182:        await executor.execute_query(
183:            """
184:            MATCH (c:Community)
185:            DETACH DELETE c
186:            """
187:        )

189:    async def determine_entity_community(
190:        self,
191:        executor: QueryExecutor,
192:        entity: EntityNode,
193:    ) -> None:
194:        # Check if the node is already part of a community
195:        records, _, _ = await executor.execute_query(
196:            """
197:            MATCH (c:Community)-[:HAS_MEMBER]->(n:Entity {uuid: $entity_uuid})
198:            RETURN
199:            """
200:            + COMMUNITY_NODE_RETURN,
201:            entity_uuid=entity.uuid,
202:        )
203:
204:        if len(records) > 0:
205:            return
206:
207:        # If the node has no community, find the mode community of surrounding entities
208:        records, _, _ = await executor.execute_query(
209:            """
210:            MATCH (c:Community)-[:HAS_MEMBER]->(m:Entity)-[:RELATES_TO]-(n:Entity {uuid: $entity_uuid})
211:            RETURN
212:            """
213:            + COMMUNITY_NODE_RETURN,

@mcp.tool()
async def delete_entity_edge(uuid: str) -> SuccessResponse | ErrorResponse:
    """Delete an entity edge from the graph memory.

    Args:
        uuid: UUID of the entity edge to delete
    """
    global graphiti_service

    if graphiti_service is None:
        return ErrorResponse(error='Graphiti service not initialized')

    try:
        client = await graphiti_service.get_client()

        # Get the entity edge by UUID
        entity_edge = await EntityEdge.get_by_uuid(client.driver, uuid)
        # Delete the edge using its delete method
        await entity_edge.delete(client.driver)
        return SuccessResponse(message=f'Entity edge with UUID {uuid} deleted successfully')
    except Exception as e:
        error_msg = str(e)
        logger.error(f'Error deleting entity edge: {error_msg}')
        return ErrorResponse(error=f'Error deleting entity edge: {error_msg}')


@mcp.tool()
async def delete_episode(uuid: str) -> SuccessResponse | ErrorResponse:
    """Delete an episode from the graph memory.

    Args:
        uuid: UUID of the episode to delete
    """
    global graphiti_service

    if graphiti_service is None:
        return ErrorResponse(error='Graphiti service not initialized')

    try:
        client = await graphiti_service.get_client()

        # Get the episodic node by UUID
        episodic_node = await EpisodicNode.get_by_uuid(client.driver, uuid)
        # Delete the node using its delete method
        await episodic_node.delete(client.driver)
        return SuccessResponse(message=f'Episode with UUID {uuid} deleted successfully')
    except Exception as e:
        error_msg = str(e)
        logger.error(f'Error deleting episode: {error_msg}')
        return ErrorResponse(error=f'Error deleting episode: {error_msg}')


@mcp.tool()
async def get_entity_edge(uuid: str) -> dict[str, Any] | ErrorResponse:
    """Get an entity edge from the graph memory by its UUID.

    Args:
        uuid: UUID of the entity edge to retrieve
    """
    global graphiti_service

    if graphiti_service is None:
        return ErrorResponse(error='Graphiti service not initialized')

    try:
        client = await graphiti_service.get_client()

        # Get the entity edge directly using the EntityEdge class method
        entity_edge = await EntityEdge.get_by_uuid(client.driver, uuid)

        # Use the format_fact_result function to serialize the edge
        # Return the Python dict directly - MCP will handle serialization
        return format_fact_result(entity_edge)
    except Exception as e:
        error_msg = str(e)
        logger.error(f'Error getting entity edge: {error_msg}')
        return ErrorResponse(error=f'Error getting entity edge: {error_msg}')


@mcp.tool()
async def get_episodes(
    group_ids: list[str] | None = None,
    max_episodes: int = 10,
) -> EpisodeSearchResponse | ErrorResponse:
    """Get episodes from the graph memory.

    Args:
        group_ids: Optional list of group IDs to filter results
        max_episodes: Maximum number of episodes to return (default: 10)
    """
    global graphiti_service

    if graphiti_service is None:
        return ErrorResponse(error='Graphiti service not initialized')

    try:
        client = await graphiti_service.get_client()

        # Use the provided group_ids or fall back to the default from config if none provided
        effective_group_ids = (
            group_ids
            if group_ids is not None
            else [config.graphiti.group_id]
            if config.graphiti.group_id
            else []
        )

        # Get episodes from the driver directly
        from graphiti_core.nodes import EpisodicNode

        if effective_group_ids:
            episodes = await EpisodicNode.get_by_group_ids(
                client.driver, effective_group_ids, limit=max_episodes
            )
        else:
            # If no group IDs, we need to use a different approach
            # For now, return empty list when no group IDs specified
            episodes = []

        if not episodes:
            return EpisodeSearchResponse(message='No episodes found', episodes=[])

        # Format the results
        episode_results = []
        for episode in episodes:
            episode_dict = {
                'uuid': episode.uuid,
                'name': episode.name,
                'content': episode.content,
                'created_at': episode.created_at.isoformat() if episode.created_at else None,
                'source': episode.source.value
                if hasattr(episode.source, 'value')
                else str(episode.source),
                'source_description': episode.source_description,
                'group_id': episode.group_id,
            }
            episode_results.append(episode_dict)

        return EpisodeSearchResponse(
            message='Episodes retrieved successfully', episodes=episode_results
        )
    except Exception as e:
        error_msg = str(e)
        logger.error(f'Error getting episodes: {error_msg}')
        return ErrorResponse(error=f'Error getting episodes: {error_msg}')


@mcp.tool()
async def clear_graph(group_ids: list[str] | None = None) -> SuccessResponse | ErrorResponse:
    """Clear all data from the graph for specified group IDs.

    Args:
        group_ids: Optional list of group IDs to clear. If not provided, clears the default group.
    """
    global graphiti_service

    if graphiti_service is None:
        return ErrorResponse(error='Graphiti service not initialized')

    try:
        client = await graphiti_service.get_client()

        # Use the provided group_ids or fall back to the default from config if none provided
        effective_group_ids = (
            group_ids or [config.graphiti.group_id] if config.graphiti.group_id else []
        )

        if not effective_group_ids:
            return ErrorResponse(error='No group IDs specified for clearing')

        # Clear data for the specified group IDs
        await clear_data(client.driver, group_ids=effective_group_ids)

        return SuccessResponse(
            message=f'Graph data cleared successfully for group IDs: {", ".join(effective_group_ids)}'
        )
    except Exception as e:
        error_msg = str(e)
        logger.error(f'Error clearing graph: {error_msg}')
        return ErrorResponse(error=f'Error clearing graph: {error_msg}')

    async def delete(self, driver: GraphDriver):
        if driver.graph_operations_interface:
            try:
                return await driver.graph_operations_interface.node_delete(self, driver)
            except NotImplementedError:
                pass

        match driver.provider:
            case GraphProvider.NEO4J:
                records, _, _ = await driver.execute_query(
                    """
                    MATCH (n {uuid: $uuid})
                    WHERE n:Entity OR n:Episodic OR n:Community
                    OPTIONAL MATCH (n)-[r]-()
                    WITH collect(r.uuid) AS edge_uuids, n
                    DETACH DELETE n
                    RETURN edge_uuids
                    """,
                    uuid=self.uuid,
                )

            case GraphProvider.KUZU:
                for label in ['Episodic', 'Community']:
                    await driver.execute_query(
                        f"""
                        MATCH (n:{label} {{uuid: $uuid}})
                        DETACH DELETE n
                        """,
                        uuid=self.uuid,
                    )
                # Entity edges are actually nodes in Kuzu, so simple `DETACH DELETE` will not work.
                # Explicitly delete the "edge" nodes first, then the entity node.
                await driver.execute_query(
                    """
                    MATCH (n:Entity {uuid: $uuid})-[:RELATES_TO]->(e:RelatesToNode_)
                    DETACH DELETE e
                    """,
                    uuid=self.uuid,
                )
                await driver.execute_query(
                    """
                    MATCH (n:Entity {uuid: $uuid})
                    DETACH DELETE n
                    """,
                    uuid=self.uuid,
                )
            case _:  # FalkorDB, Neptune
                for label in ['Entity', 'Episodic', 'Community']:
                    await driver.execute_query(
                        f"""
                        MATCH (n:{label} {{uuid: $uuid}})
                        DETACH DELETE n
                        """,
                        uuid=self.uuid,
                    )

        logger.debug(f'Deleted Node: {self.uuid}')
